     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→type Agent interface {
    14→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    15→	Name() AgentName
    16→
    17→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    18→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    19→	Type() AgentType
    20→
    21→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    22→	Description() string
    23→
    24→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    25→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    26→
    27→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none)
    28→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    29→
    30→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks
    31→	SupportsHooks() bool
    32→
    33→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin
    34→	ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
    35→
    36→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input
    37→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    38→
    39→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    40→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    41→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    42→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    43→
    44→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    45→	// Examples:
    46→	//   Claude: ~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/
    47→	//   Aider: current working directory (returns repoPath)
    48→	//   Cursor: ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/globalStorage/
    49→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    50→
    51→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file for a given
    52→	// agent session ID. Agents use different naming conventions:
    53→	//   Claude: <sessionDir>/<id>.jsonl
    54→	//   Gemini: <sessionDir>/session-<date>-<shortid>.json (searches for existing file)
    55→	// If no existing file is found, returns a sensible default path.
    56→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    57→
    58→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    59→	// Handles different formats: JSONL (Claude), SQLite (Cursor), Markdown (Aider)
    60→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    61→
    62→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    63→	// Agent handles format conversion (JSONL, SQLite, etc.)
    64→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    65→
    66→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session
    67→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    68→}
    69→
    70→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    71→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    72→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
    73→type HookSupport interface {
    74→	Agent
    75→
    76→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
    77→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
    78→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    79→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    80→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
    81→
    82→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
    83→	UninstallHooks() error
    84→
    85→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
    86→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
    87→
    88→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
    89→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
    90→}
    91→
    92→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
    93→// Each agent defines its own hook names (verbs) which become subcommands
    94→// under `entire hooks <agent>`. The actual handling is done by handlers
    95→// registered in the CLI package to avoid circular dependencies.
    96→//
    97→// This allows different agents to have completely different hook vocabularies
    98→// (e.g., Claude Code has "stop", Cursor might have "completion").
    99→type HookHandler interface {
   100→	Agent
   101→
   102→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
   103→	// These are the subcommand names that will appear under `entire hooks <agent>`.
   104→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "pre-task", "post-task", "post-todo"]
   105→	GetHookNames() []string
   106→}
   107→
   108→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   109→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   110→// to detect session activity.
   111→type FileWatcher interface {
   112→	Agent
   113→
   114→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   115→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   116→
   117→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   118→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   119→}
   120→
   121→// TranscriptAnalyzer is implemented by agents that support transcript analysis.
   122→// This allows agent-agnostic detection of work done between checkpoints.
   123→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   124→	Agent
   125→
   126→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   127→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   128→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   129→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   130→	// Use this to efficiently check if the transcript has grown since last checkpoint.
   131→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   132→
   133→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   134→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   135→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   136→	// Returns:
   137→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   138→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   139→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   140→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   141→}
   142→
   143→// TranscriptChunker is implemented by agents that support transcript chunking.
   144→// This allows agents to split large transcripts into chunks for storage (GitHub has
   145→// a 100MB blob limit) and reassemble them when reading.
   146→type TranscriptChunker interface {
   147→	Agent
   148→
   149→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
   150→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
   151→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
   152→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
   153→
   154→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
   155→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
   156→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
   157→}
   158→

bool\n\n\t// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin\n\tParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)\n\n\t// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input\n\tGetSessionID(input *HookInput) string\n\n\t// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be\n\t// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.\n\t// Examples: [\".claude\"] for Claude, [\".gemini\"] for Gemini.\n\tProtectedDirs() []string\n\n\t// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.\n\t// Examples:\n\t//   Claude: ~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/\n\t//   Aider: current working directory (returns repoPath)\n\t//   Cursor: ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/globalStorage/\n\tGetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)\n\n\t// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file for a given\n\t// agent session ID. Agents use different naming conventions:\n\t//   Claude: <sessionDir>/<id>.jsonl\n\t//   Gemini: <sessionDir>/session-<date>-<shortid>.json (searches for existing file)\n\t// If no existing file is found, returns a sensible default path.\n\tResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string\n\n\t// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.\n\t// Handles different formats: JSONL (Claude), SQLite (Cursor), Markdown (Aider)\n\tReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)\n\n\t// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.\n\t// Agent handles format conversion (JSONL, SQLite, etc.)\n\tWriteSession(session *AgentSession) error\n\n\t// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session\n\tFormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string\n}",
  "new_string": "// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.\n// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that\n// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.\npackage agent\n\nimport (\n\t\"io\"\n)\n\n// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.\n// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its\n// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.\n//\n// The interface is organized into four groups:\n//\n//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs\n//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent\n//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript\n//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase\ntype Agent interface {\n\t// --- Identity ---\n\n\t// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., \"claude-code\", \"gemini\")\n\tName() AgentName\n\n\t// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., \"Claude Code\", \"Gemini CLI\")\n\t// This is stored in metadata and trailers.\n\tType() AgentType\n\n\t// Description returns a human-readable description for UI\n\tDescription() string\n\n\t// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository\n\tDetectPresence() (bool, error)\n\n\t// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be\n\t// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.\n\t// Examples: [\".claude\"] for Claude, [\".gemini\"] for Gemini.\n\tProtectedDirs() []string\n\n\t// --- Event Mapping ---\n\n\t// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.\n\t// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.\n\t// e.g., [\"stop\", \"user-prompt-submit\", \"session-start\", \"session-end\"]\n\tHookNames() []string\n\n\t// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.\n\t// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).\n\t// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.\n\tParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)\n\n\t// --- Transcript Storage ---\n\n\t// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.\n\tReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)\n\n\t// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.\n\t// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.\n\t// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.\n\tChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)\n\n\t// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.\n\t// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).\n\tReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)\n\n\t// --- Legacy methods (to be moved to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---\n\n\t// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none)\n\t// Deprecated: will move to HookInstaller interface\n\tGetHookConfigPath() string\n\n\t// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks\n\t// Deprecated: inferred from HookInstaller implementation\n\tSupportsHooks() bool\n\n\t// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin\n\t// Deprecated: replaced by ParseHookEvent\n\tParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)\n\n\t// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input\n\t// Deprecated: extracted inside ParseHookEvent\n\tGetSessionID(input *HookInput) string\n\n\t// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.\n\t// Deprecated: will move to SessionResumer interface\n\tGetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)\n\n\t// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.\n\t// Deprecated: will move to SessionResumer interface\n\tResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string\n\n\t// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.\n\t// Deprecated: will move to SessionResumer interface\n\tReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)\n\n\t// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.\n\t// Deprecated: will move to SessionResumer interface\n\tWriteSession(session *AgentSession) error\n\n\t// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session\n\t// Deprecated: will move to SessionResumer interface\n\tFormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string\n}"
}

If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.\n\t// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.\n\tChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)\n\n\t// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.\n\t// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).\n\tReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)\n}",
  "new_string": "// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.\n// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,\n// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle\n// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.\ntype TranscriptAnalyzer interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.\n\t// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.\n\t// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.\n\t// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.\n\tGetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)\n\n\t// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.\n\t// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.\n\t// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.\n\t// Returns:\n\t//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)\n\t//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)\n\t//   - error: any error encountered during reading\n\tExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)\n\n\t// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.\n\tExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)\n\n\t// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.\n\tExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)\n}\n\n// TranscriptChunker is implemented by agents that support transcript chunking.\n// Deprecated: ChunkTranscript and ReassembleTranscript are now part of the core Agent interface.\n// This interface is kept for backward compatibility during migration.\ntype TranscriptChunker interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.\n\tChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)\n\n\t// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.\n\tReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)\n}\n\n// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific\n// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).\n// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.\ntype TranscriptPreparer interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// PrepareTranscript ensures the transcript is ready to read.\n\t// For Claude Code, this waits for the async transcript flush to complete.\n\tPrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error\n}\n\n// TokenCalculator provides token usage calculation for a session.\n// The framework calls this during step save and checkpoint if implemented.\ntype TokenCalculator interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given offset.\n\tCalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)\n}\n\n// SessionResumer is implemented by agents that support session resumption (rewind --resume).\ntype SessionResumer interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// GetSessionDir returns where the agent stores session data for this repo.\n\tGetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)\n\n\t// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.\n\tResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string\n\n\t// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.\n\tReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)\n\n\t// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.\n\tWriteSession(session *AgentSession) error\n\n\t// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.\n\tFormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string\n}\n\n// HookInstaller is implemented by agents where hooks can be auto-installed.\ntype HookInstaller interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// GetHookConfigPath returns path to the agent's hook config file.\n\tGetHookConfigPath() string\n\n\t// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.\n\tInstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)\n\n\t// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks.\n\tUninstallHooks() error\n\n\t// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed.\n\tAreHooksInstalled() bool\n\n\t// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports.\n\tGetSupportedHooks() []HookType\n}"
}

    50→// DetectPresence checks if Claude Code is configured in the repository.
    51→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .claude directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .claude directory
    61→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(claudeDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .claude/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Claude's hook config file.
    74→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".claude/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Claude Code supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    84→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   103→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   110→
   111→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   112→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   113→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   114→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   115→		}
   116→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   117→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   120→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   121→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   122→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   123→		}
   124→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   125→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   126→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   127→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   128→
   129→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   130→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   135→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   136→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   137→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   138→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   139→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   140→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   141→		}
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return input, nil
   145→}

   147→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   148→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   149→	return input.SessionID
   150→}
   151→
   152→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Claude session file.
   153→// Claude names files directly as <id>.jsonl.
   154→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   155→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Claude uses for config/state.
   159→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".claude"} }
   160→
   161→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   162→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   163→	// Check for test environment override
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   174→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   178→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   179→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   180→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   181→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   182→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   186→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   192→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   198→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   199→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   200→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   201→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   202→		NativeData:    data,
   203→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   204→	}, nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   208→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   209→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   210→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→	if session == nil {
   212→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   216→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   217→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   221→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   225→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   229→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   237→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   238→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   239→}
   240→
   241→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   242→
   243→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   244→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   245→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   246→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   247→		return ""
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return ""
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   256→}
   257→
   258→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   259→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   260→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   261→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   262→	if session == nil {
   263→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   267→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	if uuid == "" {
   271→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   272→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   273→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   274→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   275→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   276→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   277→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   278→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   279→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   280→		}, nil
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   284→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   290→
   291→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   297→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   298→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   299→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   300→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   301→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   302→		NativeData:    newData,
   303→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   304→	}, nil
   305→}
   306→
   307→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   308→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   309→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   310→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   311→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   312→		return "", false
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		return "", false
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   324→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   325→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   326→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   327→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   328→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   329→	})
   330→}
   331→
   332→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   333→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   334→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   335→
   336→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   337→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   338→}
   339→
   340→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   341→
   342→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   343→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   344→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   345→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   346→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   347→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   348→	if path == "" {
   349→		return 0, nil
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   355→			return 0, nil
   356→		}
   357→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   358→	}
   359→	defer file.Close()
   360→
   361→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   362→	lineCount := 0
   363→
   364→	for {
   365→		_, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   366→		if err != nil {
   367→			if err == io.EOF {
   368→				break
   369→			}
   370→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   371→		}
   372→		lineCount++
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return lineCount, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   379→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   380→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   381→// Returns:
   382→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   383→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   384→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   385→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   386→	if path == "" {
   387→		return nil, 0, nil
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   391→	if openErr != nil {
   392→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   393→	}
   394→	defer file.Close()
   395→
   396→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   397→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   398→	lineNum := 0
   399→
   400→	for {
   401→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   402→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   403→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   404→		}
   405→
   406→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   407→			lineNum++
   408→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   409→				var line TranscriptLine
   410→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   411→					lines = append(lines, line)
   412→				}
   413→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   414→			}
   415→		}
   416→
   417→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   418→			break
   419→		}
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   423→}
   424→
   425→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   426→
   427→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   428→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   429→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   430→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   431→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   432→	if err != nil {
   433→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   434→	}
   435→	return chunks, nil
   436→}
   437→
   438→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   439→//
   440→//nolint:unparam // error return is required by interface, kept for consistency
   441→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   442→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   443→}

   138→		preTaskCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code pre-task"

   140→		postTodoCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-todo"
   141→	} else {

   145→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"

   210→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   211→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)

   214→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)

   232→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   233→	if err != nil {

   238→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)

   243→
   244→// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}
   266→
   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   268→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., "Notification", "SubagentStop")
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse []ClaudeHookMatcher
   298→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   299→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   300→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", &stop)
   301→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", &userPromptSubmit)
   302→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   303→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)
   304→
   305→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   306→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   307→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   308→	stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   309→	userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   310→	preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   311→	postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   312→
   313→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   314→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   315→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   316→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   317→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   318→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   319→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   320→
   321→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   322→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   323→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   324→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   325→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   326→			rawPermissions = nil
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   331→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   332→			var denyRules []string
   333→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   334→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   335→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   336→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   337→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   338→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   339→					}
   340→				}
   341→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   342→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   343→					if err == nil {
   344→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   345→					}
   346→				} else {
   347→					// Remove empty deny array
   348→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   349→				}
   350→			}
   351→		}
   352→
   353→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   354→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   355→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   356→			if err == nil {
   357→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   358→			}
   359→		} else {
   360→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   361→		}
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   365→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   366→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   367→		if err != nil {
   368→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   369→		}
   370→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   371→	} else {
   372→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Write back
   376→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   381→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	return nil
   384→}
   385→
   386→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   387→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   388→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   389→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   390→	if err != nil {
   391→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   392→	}
   393→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   394→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   395→	if err != nil {
   396→		return false
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   400→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   401→		return false
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   405→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   406→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   407→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   408→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   409→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   410→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   411→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   412→}
   413→
   414→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   415→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   416→	return []agent.HookType{
   417→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   418→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   419→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   420→		agent.HookStop,
   421→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   422→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   423→	}
   424→}
   425→
   426→// Helper functions for hook management
   427→
   428→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   431→			if hook.Command == command {
   432→				return true
   433→			}
   434→		}
   435→	}
   436→	return false
   437→}
   438→
   439→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   440→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   441→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   442→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   443→				if hook.Command == command {
   444→					return true
   445→				}
   446→			}
   447→		}
   448→	}
   449→	return false
   450→}
   451→
   452→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   453→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   454→		Type:    "command",
   455→		Command: command,
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   459→	if matcherName == "" {
   460→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   461→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   462→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   463→				return matchers
   464→			}
   465→		}
   466→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   467→			Matcher: "",
   468→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   469→		})
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   473→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   474→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   475→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   476→			return matchers
   477→		}
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   481→		Matcher: matcherName,
   482→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   483→	})
   484→}
   485→
   486→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   487→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   488→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   489→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   490→			return true
   491→		}
   492→	}
   493→	return false
   494→}
   495→
   496→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   497→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   498→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   499→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   500→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   501→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   502→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   503→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   504→			}
   505→		}
   506→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   507→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   508→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   509→			result = append(result, matcher)
   510→		}
   511→	}
   512→	return result
   513→}
   514→
   515→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   516→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   517→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   518→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   519→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   520→}
   521→

    24→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    25→type hookInputData struct {
    26→	agent     agent.Agent
    27→	input     *agent.HookInput
    28→	sessionID string
    29→}
    30→
    31→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    32→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    33→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    34→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    40→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    46→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    47→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    48→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    49→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    50→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    51→	)
    52→
    53→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    54→	if sessionID == "" {
    55→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    59→	return &hookInputData{
    60→		agent:     ag,
    61→		input:     input,
    62→		sessionID: sessionID,
    63→	}, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    67→func captureInitialState() error {
    68→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    69→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return err
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    75→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    76→		return err
    77→	}
    78→

    80→	strat := GetStrategy()

    13→
    14→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    15→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    16→
    17→// Type aliases for internal use.
    18→type (
    19→	userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
    20→	assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage
    21→	toolInput        = transcript.ToolInput
    22→)
    23→
    24→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes
    25→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
    26→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    27→	for _, line := range lines {
    28→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
    29→		if err != nil {
    30→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
    31→		}
    32→		buf.Write(data)
    33→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
    34→	}
    35→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript
    39→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
    40→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    41→	var files []string
    42→
    43→	for _, line := range lines {
    44→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
    45→			continue
    46→		}
    47→
    48→		var msg assistantMessage
    49→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    50→			continue
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    54→			if block.Type != "tool_use" {
    55→				continue
    56→			}
    57→
    58→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    59→			isModifyTool := false
    60→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    61→				if block.Name == name {
    62→					isModifyTool = true
    63→					break
    64→				}
    65→			}
    66→
    67→			if !isModifyTool {
    68→				continue
    69→			}
    70→
    71→			var input toolInput
    72→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
    73→				continue
    74→			}
    75→
    76→			file := input.FilePath
    77→			if file == "" {
    78→				file = input.NotebookPath
    79→			}
    80→
    81→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
    82→				fileSet[file] = true
    83→				files = append(files, file)
    84→			}
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	return files
    89→}
    90→

   116→						}

   119→			}

   800→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   801→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   802→//
   803→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   804→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   805→//
   806→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   807→// with whatever data is available.
   808→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   809→	const (
   810→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   811→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   812→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   813→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   814→	)
   815→
   816→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   817→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   818→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   819→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   820→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   821→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   822→			)
   823→			return
   824→		}
   825→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   826→	}
   827→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   828→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   829→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   830→	)
   831→}
   832→
   833→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   834→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   835→// close to hookStartTime.
   836→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   837→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   838→	if err != nil {
   839→		return false
   840→	}
   841→	defer f.Close()
   842→
   843→	// Seek to tail
   844→	info, err := f.Stat()
   845→	if err != nil {
   846→		return false
   847→	}
   848→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   849→	if offset < 0 {
   850→		offset = 0
   851→	}
   852→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   853→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   854→		return false
   855→	}
   856→
   857→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   858→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   859→	for _, line := range lines {
   860→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   861→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   862→			continue
   863→		}
   864→
   865→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   866→		var entry struct {
   867→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   868→		}
   869→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   870→			continue
   871→		}
   872→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   873→		if err != nil {
   874→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   875→			if err != nil {
   876→				continue
   877→			}
   878→		}
   879→

    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.

    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {

    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.

    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    51→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .gemini directory
    61→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    74→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    84→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   103→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   110→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   111→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   112→		if raw.Source != "" {
   113→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   114→		}
   115→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   116→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   117→		}
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   120→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   121→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   122→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   123→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   127→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   128→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   129→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   130→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   131→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   132→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   133→		}
   134→
   135→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   136→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   137→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   138→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   139→		}
   140→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   141→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	return input, nil
   152→}
   153→
   154→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   155→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   156→	return input.SessionID
   157→}
   158→
   159→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
   160→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
   161→
   162→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
   163→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
   164→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
   165→// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
   166→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   167→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
   168→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
   169→	shortID := agentSessionID
   170→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   171→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
   175→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
   176→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
   177→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
   178→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Fallback: construct a default path
   182→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   183→}
   184→
   185→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   186→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   187→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   188→	// Check for test environment override
   189→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   190→		return override, nil
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
   199→	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
   200→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   204→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   205→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   206→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   207→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Read the raw JSON file
   211→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   217→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   220→		modifiedFiles = nil
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   224→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   225→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   226→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   227→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   228→		NativeData:    data,
   229→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   230→	}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   234→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   235→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   236→	if session == nil {
   237→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   241→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   246→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   250→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Write the raw JSON data
   254→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	return nil
   259→}
   260→
   261→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   262→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   263→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   264→}
   265→
   266→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   267→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   268→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   269→
   270→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   271→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   275→
   276→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   277→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   278→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   279→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   280→	if path == "" {
   281→		return 0, nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   287→			return 0, nil
   288→		}
   289→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	if len(data) == 0 {
   293→		return 0, nil
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   302→}
   303→
   304→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   305→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   306→// Returns:
   307→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   308→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   309→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   310→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   311→	if path == "" {
   312→		return nil, 0, nil
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   316→	if readErr != nil {
   317→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   318→			return nil, 0, nil
   319→		}
   320→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	if len(data) == 0 {
   324→		return nil, 0, nil
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   328→	if parseErr != nil {
   329→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   333→
   334→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   335→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   336→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   337→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   338→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   339→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   340→			continue
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Process tool calls in this message
   344→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   345→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   346→			isModifyTool := false
   347→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   348→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   349→					isModifyTool = true
   350→					break
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			if !isModifyTool {
   355→				continue
   356→			}
   357→
   358→			// Extract file path from args map
   359→			var file string
   360→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   361→				file = fp
   362→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   363→				file = p
   364→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   365→				file = fn
   366→			}
   367→
   368→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   369→				fileSet[file] = true
   370→				files = append(files, file)
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   379→
   380→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   381→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   382→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   383→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   384→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   385→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   386→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   387→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   388→		if chunkErr != nil {
   389→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   390→		}
   391→		return chunks, nil
   392→	}
   393→
   394→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   395→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	var chunks [][]byte
   399→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   400→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   401→
   402→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   403→		// Marshal message to get its size
   404→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   405→		if err != nil {
   406→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   407→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   408→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   409→			)
   410→			continue
   411→		}
   412→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   413→
   414→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   415→			// Save current chunk
   416→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   417→			if err != nil {
   418→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   419→			}
   420→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   421→
   422→			// Start new chunk
   423→			currentMessages = nil
   424→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   425→		}
   426→
   427→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   428→		currentSize += msgSize
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// Add the last chunk
   432→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   433→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   434→		if err != nil {
   435→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   436→		}
   437→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   441→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   442→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	return chunks, nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   449→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   450→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   451→
   452→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   453→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   454→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   455→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   456→		}
   457→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   461→	if err != nil {
   462→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   463→	}
   464→	return result, nil
   465→}
   466→

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"strings"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// Transcript message type constants - aliases to transcript package for local use.
    18→const (
    19→	transcriptTypeUser      = transcript.TypeUser
    20→	transcriptTypeAssistant = transcript.TypeAssistant
    21→	contentTypeText         = transcript.ContentTypeText
    22→	contentTypeToolUse      = transcript.ContentTypeToolUse
    23→)
    24→
    25→// PromptResponsePair represents a user prompt and the assistant's responses.
    26→// Used for extracting all interactions from a transcript.
    27→type PromptResponsePair struct {
    28→	Prompt    string
    29→	Responses []string // Multiple responses can occur between tool calls
    30→	Files     []string
    31→}
    32→
    33→// ExtractAllPromptResponses extracts all user prompt and assistant response pairs from a transcript.
    34→// Returns pairs in chronological order (oldest first).
    35→// Each pair contains the user's prompt, assistant's text response, and files modified.
    36→func ExtractAllPromptResponses(transcript []transcriptLine) []PromptResponsePair {
    37→	var pairs []PromptResponsePair
    38→
    39→	// Find all user prompts (messages with string content, not tool results)
    40→	var userIndices []int
    41→	for i, line := range transcript {
    42→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeUser {
    43→			var msg userMessage
    44→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    45→				continue
    46→			}
    47→			// Only count messages with string content (real user prompts)
    48→			if _, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
    49→				userIndices = append(userIndices, i)
    50→			} else if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
    51→				// Check if array has text blocks (also a real user prompt)
    52→				for _, item := range arr {
    53→					if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
    54→						if m["type"] == contentTypeText {
    55→							userIndices = append(userIndices, i)
    56→							break
    57→						}
    58→					}
    59→				}
    60→			}
    61→		}
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	// For each user prompt, extract the prompt text, following assistant response, and files
    65→	for idx, userIdx := range userIndices {
    66→		// Determine the range for this prompt's conversation
    67→		endIdx := len(transcript)
    68→		if idx < len(userIndices)-1 {
    69→			endIdx = userIndices[idx+1]
    70→		}
    71→
    72→		// Extract prompt text
    73→		prompt := extractUserPromptAt(transcript, userIdx)
    74→		if prompt == "" {
    75→			continue
    76→		}
    77→
    78→		// Extract assistant responses and files from this range
    79→		slice := transcript[userIdx:endIdx]
    80→		responses := extractAssistantResponses(slice)
    81→		files := extractModifiedFiles(slice)
    82→
    83→		pairs = append(pairs, PromptResponsePair{
    84→			Prompt:    prompt,
    85→			Responses: responses,
    86→			Files:     files,
    87→		})
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	return pairs
    91→}
    92→
    93→// extractUserPromptAt extracts the user prompt at the given index.
    94→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
    95→func extractUserPromptAt(lines []transcriptLine, idx int) string {
    96→	if idx >= len(lines) || lines[idx].Type != transcriptTypeUser {
    97→		return ""
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	return transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[idx].Message)
   101→}
   102→
   103→// extractAssistantResponses collects all assistant text blocks from the given transcript slice.
   104→// A single prompt can trigger multiple assistant responses interspersed with tool calls.
   105→func extractAssistantResponses(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   106→	var texts []string
   107→	for _, line := range transcript {
   108→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   109→			var msg assistantMessage
   110→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   111→				continue
   112→			}
   113→
   114→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   115→				if block.Type == contentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   116→					texts = append(texts, block.Text)
   117→				}
   118→			}
   119→		}
   120→	}
   121→	return texts
   122→}
   123→
   124→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user messages from the transcript in order.
   125→// Only considers messages with string content (not tool results).
   126→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   127→func extractUserPrompts(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   128→	var prompts []string
   129→	for i := range transcript {
   130→		if transcript[i].Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   131→			continue
   132→		}
   133→		var msg userMessage
   134→		if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   135→			continue
   136→		}
   137→
   138→		// Handle string content
   139→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   140→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(str))
   141→			continue
   142→		}
   143→

readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tevent := &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}\n\tif raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\tevent.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID\n\t}\n\treturn event, nil\n}\n\n// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.\nfunc readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read hook input: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"empty hook input\")\n\t}\n\tvar result T\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hook input: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &result, nil\n}\n\n// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---\n\n// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress\n// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.\nconst stopHookSentinel = \"hooks claude-code stop\"\n\n// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.\n// Falls back silently after a timeout.\nfunc waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {\n\tconst (\n\t\tmaxWait      = 3 * time.Second\n\t\tpollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond\n\t\ttailBytes    = 4096\n\t\tmaxSkew      = 2 * time.Second\n\t)\n\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"agent.claudecode\")\n\tdeadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)\n\tfor time.Now().Before(deadline) {\n\t\tif checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"transcript flush sentinel found\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.Duration(\"wait\", time.Since(hookStartTime)),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\ttime.Sleep(pollInterval)\n\t}\n\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding\",\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"timeout\", maxWait),\n\t)\n}\n\n// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.\nfunc checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {\n\tf, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tdefer f.Close()\n\n\tinfo, err := f.Stat()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\toffset := info.Size() - tailBytes\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\tbuf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)\n\tif _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(string(buf), \"\\n\")\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\tline = strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif line == \"\" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar entry struct {\n\t\t\tTimestamp string `json:\"timestamp\"`\n\t\t}\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcutoff := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)\n\t\tif ts.After(cutoff) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n"
