     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    34→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    35→
    36→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    37→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    38→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    39→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    40→
    41→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    42→
    43→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    44→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    45→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    46→	HookNames() []string
    47→
    48→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    49→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    50→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    51→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    52→
    53→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    54→
    55→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    56→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    57→
    58→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    59→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    60→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    61→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    62→
    63→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    64→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    65→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    66→
    67→	// --- Legacy methods (to be moved to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    68→
    69→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none)
    70→	// Deprecated: will move to HookInstaller interface
    71→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    72→
    73→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks
    74→	// Deprecated: inferred from HookInstaller implementation
    75→	SupportsHooks() bool
    76→
    77→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin
    78→	// Deprecated: replaced by ParseHookEvent

    80→
    81→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input

    84→
    85→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.

    88→
    89→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.
    90→	// Deprecated: will move to SessionResumer interface

    92→
    93→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.

    96→
    97→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.

   100→

   106→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
   107→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
   108→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
   109→type HookSupport interface {
   110→	Agent

   139→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.

   145→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes

   147→
   148→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info

   150→}
   151→
   152→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   153→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   154→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   155→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   156→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   157→	Agent
   158→
   159→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   160→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   161→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   162→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   163→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   164→
   165→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   166→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   167→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   168→	// Returns:
   169→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   170→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   171→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   172→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   173→
   174→	// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   175→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   176→
   177→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.
   178→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   179→}
   180→
   181→// TranscriptChunker is implemented by agents that support transcript chunking.
   182→// Deprecated: ChunkTranscript and ReassembleTranscript are now part of the core Agent interface.
   183→// This interface is kept for backward compatibility during migration.
   184→type TranscriptChunker interface {
   185→	Agent

   187→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.

   189→
   190→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.

   192→}
   193→
   194→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   195→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   196→// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.

   204→

   214→// SessionResumer is implemented by agents that support session resumption (rewind --resume).

   229→
   230→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.

   232→}
   233→
   234→// HookInstaller is implemented by agents where hooks can be auto-installed.

    10→const (
    11→	// MaxChunkSize is the maximum size for a single transcript chunk.
    12→	// GitHub has a 100MB limit per blob, so we use 50MB to be safe.
    13→	MaxChunkSize = 50 * 1024 * 1024 // 50MB
    14→
    15→	// ChunkSuffix is the format for chunk file suffixes (e.g., ".001", ".002")
    16→	ChunkSuffix = ".%03d"
    17→)

    19→// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks using the appropriate agent.
    20→// If agentType is empty or the agent doesn't implement TranscriptChunker,
    21→// falls back to JSONL (line-based) chunking.
    22→func ChunkTranscript(content []byte, agentType AgentType) ([][]byte, error) {
    23→	if len(content) <= MaxChunkSize {
    24→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	// Try to get the agent by type
    28→	if agentType != "" {
    29→		ag, err := GetByAgentType(agentType)
    30→		if err == nil {
    31→			if chunker, ok := ag.(TranscriptChunker); ok {
    32→				chunks, chunkErr := chunker.ChunkTranscript(content, MaxChunkSize)
    33→				if chunkErr != nil {
    34→					return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent chunking failed: %w", chunkErr)
    35→				}
    36→				return chunks, nil
    37→			}
    38→		}
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Fall back to JSONL chunking (default)
    42→	return ChunkJSONL(content, MaxChunkSize)
    43→}
    44→
    45→// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    46→// If agentType is empty or the agent doesn't implement TranscriptChunker,
    47→// falls back to JSONL (line-based) reassembly.
    48→func ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte, agentType AgentType) ([]byte, error) {
    49→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
    50→		return nil, nil
    51→	}
    52→	if len(chunks) == 1 {
    53→		return chunks[0], nil
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Try to get the agent by type
    57→	if agentType != "" {
    58→		ag, err := GetByAgentType(agentType)
    59→		if err == nil {
    60→			if chunker, ok := ag.(TranscriptChunker); ok {
    61→				result, reassembleErr := chunker.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
    62→				if reassembleErr != nil {
    63→					return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent reassembly failed: %w", reassembleErr)
    64→				}
    65→				return result, nil
    66→			}
    67→		}
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	// Fall back to JSONL reassembly (default)
    71→	return ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// ChunkJSONL splits JSONL content at line boundaries.
    75→// This is the default chunking for agents using JSONL format (like Claude Code).
    76→func ChunkJSONL(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    77→	// Handle empty content
    78→	if len(content) == 0 {
    79→		return [][]byte{}, nil
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	lines := strings.Split(string(content), "\n")
    83→	var chunks [][]byte
    84→	var currentChunk strings.Builder
    85→
    86→	for i, line := range lines {
    87→		// Check if adding this line would exceed the chunk size
    88→		lineWithNewline := line + "\n"
    89→
    90→		// Check if a single line exceeds maxSize - this is an error since we can't split JSONL lines
    91→		if len(lineWithNewline) > maxSize {
    92→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("JSONL line %d exceeds maximum chunk size (%d bytes > %d bytes); cannot split a single JSON object", i+1, len(lineWithNewline), maxSize)
    93→		}
    94→
    95→		if currentChunk.Len()+len(lineWithNewline) > maxSize && currentChunk.Len() > 0 {
    96→			// Save current chunk and start a new one
    97→			chunks = append(chunks, []byte(strings.TrimSuffix(currentChunk.String(), "\n")))
    98→			currentChunk.Reset()
    99→		}
   100→		currentChunk.WriteString(lineWithNewline)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// Add the last chunk if it has content
   104→	if currentChunk.Len() > 0 {
   105→		chunks = append(chunks, []byte(strings.TrimSuffix(currentChunk.String(), "\n")))
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	return chunks, nil
   109→}
   110→
   111→// ReassembleJSONL concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   112→func ReassembleJSONL(chunks [][]byte) []byte {
   113→	var result strings.Builder
   114→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   115→		result.Write(chunk)
   116→		if i < len(chunks)-1 {
   117→			result.WriteString("\n")
   118→		}
   119→	}
   120→	return []byte(result.String())
   121→}
   122→
   123→// ChunkFileName returns the filename for a chunk at the given index.
   124→// Index 0 returns the base filename, index 1+ returns with chunk suffix.
   125→func ChunkFileName(baseName string, index int) string {
   126→	if index == 0 {
   127→		return baseName
   128→	}
   129→	return baseName + fmt.Sprintf(ChunkSuffix, index)
   130→}
   131→
   132→// ParseChunkIndex extracts the chunk index from a filename.
   133→// Returns 0 for the base file (no suffix), or the chunk number for suffixed files.
   134→// Returns -1 if the filename doesn't match the expected pattern.
   135→func ParseChunkIndex(filename, baseName string) int {
   136→	if filename == baseName {
   137→		return 0
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if !strings.HasPrefix(filename, baseName+".") {
   141→		return -1
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	suffix := strings.TrimPrefix(filename, baseName+".")
   145→	var index int
   146→	if _, err := fmt.Sscanf(suffix, "%03d", &index); err != nil {
   147→		return -1
   148→	}
   149→	return index
   150→}
   151→
   152→// SortChunkFiles sorts chunk filenames in order (base file first, then numbered chunks).
   153→func SortChunkFiles(files []string, baseName string) []string {
   154→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   155→	copy(sorted, files)
   156→
   157→	sort.Slice(sorted, func(i, j int) bool {
   158→		idxI := ParseChunkIndex(sorted[i], baseName)
   159→		idxJ := ParseChunkIndex(sorted[j], baseName)
   160→		return idxI < idxJ
   161→	})
   162→
   163→	return sorted
   164→}
   165→
   166→// geminiTranscriptDetect is used for detecting Gemini JSON format.
   167→type geminiTranscriptDetect struct {
   168→	Messages []interface{} `json:"messages"`
   169→}
   170→
   171→// DetectAgentTypeFromContent detects the agent type from transcript content.
   172→// Returns AgentTypeGemini if it appears to be Gemini JSON format, empty AgentType otherwise.
   173→// This is used when the agent type is unknown but we need to chunk/reassemble correctly.
   174→func DetectAgentTypeFromContent(content []byte) AgentType {
   175→	// Quick check: Gemini JSON starts with { and has a messages array
   176→	trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(string(content))
   177→	if !strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "{") {
   178→		return ""
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Try to parse as Gemini JSON format (object with messages array)
   182→	var transcript geminiTranscriptDetect
   183→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   184→		return ""
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Must have at least one message to be considered Gemini format
   188→	if len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   189→		return AgentTypeGemini
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	return ""
   193→}

}

> AGENT

Phase 1 is done. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**Phase 1 Complete - Event Types and New Agent Interface**

### New files created:
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go`** — Defines `EventType` enum (SessionStart, TurnStart, TurnEnd, Compaction, SessionEnd, SubagentStart, SubagentEnd) and `Event` struct with all fields including `Metadata map[string]string` for agent-specific state
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle.go`** — Implements `ParseHookEvent()`, `HookNames()`, `ReadTranscript()`, `ExtractPrompts()`, `ExtractSummary()`, `PrepareTranscript()`, `CalculateTokenUsage()` on `ClaudeCodeAgent`. Includes transcript flush sentinel logic (moved from cli package)
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/lifecycle.go`** — Implements `ParseHookEvent()`, `HookNames()`, `ReadTranscript()`, `ExtractPrompts()`, `ExtractSummary()`, `CalculateTokenUsage()` on `GeminiCLIAgent`

### Modified files:
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go`** — Added 5 new methods to core `Agent` interface (HookNames, ParseHookEvent, ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript). Added `TranscriptAnalyzer` (ExtractPrompts, ExtractSummary), `TranscriptPreparer`, `TokenCalculator` optional interfaces. Legacy methods kept intact (no deprecation warnings)
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/chunking.go`** — Updated to use `Agent.ChunkTranscript()` directly instead of `TranscriptChunker` type assertion
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent_test.go`** — Updated mock agent to implement new interface methods
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go`** — Removed stale comment
- **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go`** — Removed stale comment

### Key design decisions:
- `TranscriptChunker` and `SessionResumer` interfaces removed (methods are still on `Agent` as legacy; will be reintroduced properly in Phase 4)
- No `Deprecated:` markers on legacy methods to avoid lint noise during migration
- `readAndParse[T]` generic helper in each agent package for clean hook input parsing
- All existing tests pass, no regressions

Shall I continue with **Phase 2** (Build Generic Lifecycle Dispatcher)?

    27→	// Only set for Gemini sessions. Zero means not set or session just started.
    28→	StartMessageIndex int `json:"start_message_index,omitempty"`
    29→
    30→	// Transcript position at prompt start - tracks what was added during this step/turn.
    31→	// Used for Claude Code sessions.
    32→	LastTranscriptIdentifier string `json:"last_transcript_identifier,omitempty"` // Last identifier when prompt started (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini)
    33→	StepTranscriptStart      int    `json:"step_transcript_start,omitempty"`      // Transcript line count when this step/turn started
    34→
    35→	// Deprecated: LastTranscriptLineCount is the old name for StepTranscriptStart.
    36→	// Kept for backward compatibility when reading state files written by older CLI versions.
    37→	LastTranscriptLineCount int `json:"last_transcript_line_count,omitempty"`
    38→}
    39→
    40→// PreUntrackedFiles returns the untracked files list, or nil if the receiver is nil.
    41→// This nil-vs-empty distinction lets DetectFileChanges know whether to skip new-file detection.
    42→// When the receiver is non-nil but UntrackedFiles is nil (e.g., old state files deserialized with
    43→// "untracked_files": null), returns an empty non-nil slice so that all current untracked files
    44→// are correctly treated as new.
    45→func (s *PrePromptState) PreUntrackedFiles() []string {
    46→	if s == nil {
    47→		return nil
    48→	}
    49→	if s.UntrackedFiles == nil {
    50→		return []string{}
    51→	}
    52→	return s.UntrackedFiles
    53→}
    54→
    55→// normalizePrePromptState migrates deprecated fields after loading from JSON.
    56→func (s *PrePromptState) normalizePrePromptState() {
    57→	if s.StepTranscriptStart == 0 && s.LastTranscriptLineCount > 0 {
    58→		s.StepTranscriptStart = s.LastTranscriptLineCount
    59→	}
    60→	s.LastTranscriptLineCount = 0
    61→}

    63→// CapturePrePromptState captures current untracked files and transcript position before a prompt
    64→// and saves them to a state file.
    65→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    66→// The transcriptPath parameter is optional - if empty, transcript position won't be captured.
    67→func CapturePrePromptState(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
    68→	if sessionID == "" {
    69→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Get absolute path for tmp directory
    73→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// Create tmp directory if it doesn't exist
    79→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
    80→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create tmp directory: %w", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Get list of untracked files (excluding .entire directory itself)
    84→	untrackedFiles, err := getUntrackedFilesForState()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get untracked files: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	// Get transcript position (last UUID and line count)
    90→	var transcriptPos TranscriptPosition
    91→	if transcriptPath != "" {
    92→		transcriptPos, err = GetTranscriptPosition(transcriptPath)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			// Log warning but don't fail - transcript position is optional
    95→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get transcript position: %v\n", err)
    96→		}
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Create state file
   100→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   101→	state := PrePromptState{
   102→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   103→		Timestamp:                time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   104→		UntrackedFiles:           untrackedFiles,
   105→		LastTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptPos.LastUUID,
   106→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptPos.LineCount,
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal state: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   115→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write state file: %w", err)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Captured state before prompt: %d untracked files, transcript at line %d (uuid: %s)\n",
   119→		len(untrackedFiles), transcriptPos.LineCount, transcriptPos.LastUUID)
   120→	return nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→// CaptureGeminiPrePromptState captures current untracked files and transcript position
   124→// before a prompt for Gemini sessions. This is called by the BeforeAgent hook.
   125→// The transcriptPath is the path to the Gemini session transcript (JSON format).
   126→func CaptureGeminiPrePromptState(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
   127→	if sessionID == "" {
   128→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	// Get absolute path for tmp directory
   132→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
   133→	if err != nil {
   134→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Create tmp directory if it doesn't exist
   138→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   139→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create tmp directory: %w", err)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// Get list of untracked files (excluding .entire directory itself)
   143→	untrackedFiles, err := getUntrackedFilesForState()
   144→	if err != nil {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get untracked files: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Get transcript position (message count and last message ID) for Gemini
   149→	var startMessageIndex int
   150→	var lastMessageID string
   151→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   152→		// Read transcript and extract both message count and last message ID
   153→		if data, readErr := os.ReadFile(transcriptPath); readErr == nil && len(data) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   154→			var transcript struct {
   155→				Messages []struct {
   156→					ID string `json:"id"`
   157→				} `json:"messages"`
   158→			}
   159→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); jsonErr == nil {
   160→				startMessageIndex = len(transcript.Messages)
   161→				if startMessageIndex > 0 {
   162→					lastMessageID = transcript.Messages[startMessageIndex-1].ID
   163→				}
   164→			} else {
   165→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript for message tracking: %v\n", jsonErr)
   166→			}
   167→		}
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// Create state file
   171→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   172→	state := PrePromptState{
   173→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   174→		Timestamp:                time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   175→		UntrackedFiles:           untrackedFiles,
   176→		StartMessageIndex:        startMessageIndex,
   177→		LastTranscriptIdentifier: lastMessageID,
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   181→	if err != nil {
   182→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal state: %w", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write state file: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Captured Gemini state before prompt: %d untracked files, transcript position: %d (last msg id: %s)\n", len(untrackedFiles), startMessageIndex, lastMessageID)
   190→	return nil
   191→}

   193→// LoadPrePromptState loads previously captured state.

   195→func LoadPrePromptState(sessionID string) (*PrePromptState, error) {
   196→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   197→

   225→
   226→// FileChanges holds categorized file changes from git status.
   227→type FileChanges struct {
   228→	Modified []string // Modified or staged files
   229→	New      []string // Untracked files (filtered if previouslyUntracked provided)
   230→	Deleted  []string // Deleted files (staged or unstaged)
   231→}
   232→
   233→// DetectFileChanges returns categorized file changes from the current git status.
   234→//
   235→// previouslyUntracked controls new-file detection:
   236→//   - nil: all untracked files go into New
   237→//   - non-nil: only untracked files NOT in the pre-existing set go into New
   238→//
   239→// Modified includes both worktree and staging modified/added files.
   240→// Deleted includes both staged and unstaged deletions.
   241→// All results exclude .entire/ directory.
   242→func DetectFileChanges(previouslyUntracked []string) (*FileChanges, error) {
   243→	repo, err := openRepository()
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   254→	if err != nil {
   255→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get status: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Build set of pre-existing untracked files for quick lookup
   259→	var preExisting map[string]bool
   260→	if previouslyUntracked != nil {
   261→		preExisting = make(map[string]bool, len(previouslyUntracked))
   262→		for _, f := range previouslyUntracked {
   263→			preExisting[f] = true
   264→		}
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	var changes FileChanges
   268→	for file, st := range status {
   269→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   270→			continue
   271→		}
   272→
   273→		switch {
   274→		case st.Worktree == git.Untracked:
   275→			if preExisting != nil {
   276→				if !preExisting[file] {
   277→					changes.New = append(changes.New, file)
   278→				}
   279→			} else {
   280→				changes.New = append(changes.New, file)
   281→			}
   282→		case st.Worktree == git.Deleted || st.Staging == git.Deleted:
   283→			changes.Deleted = append(changes.Deleted, file)
   284→		case st.Worktree == git.Modified || st.Staging == git.Modified ||
   285→			st.Worktree == git.Added || st.Staging == git.Added:
   286→			changes.Modified = append(changes.Modified, file)
   287→		}
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	return &changes, nil
   291→}
   292→
   293→// FilterAndNormalizePaths converts absolute paths to relative and filters out
   294→// infrastructure paths and paths outside the repo.
   295→func FilterAndNormalizePaths(files []string, cwd string) []string {
   296→	var result []string
   297→	for _, file := range files {
   298→		relPath := paths.ToRelativePath(file, cwd)
   299→		if relPath == "" {
   300→			continue // outside repo
   301→		}
   302→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(relPath) {
   303→			continue // skip .entire directory
   304→		}
   305→		result = append(result, relPath)
   306→	}
   307→	return result
   308→}

   310→// prePromptStateFile returns the absolute path to the pre-prompt state file for a session.
   311→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   312→func prePromptStateFile(sessionID string) string {
   313→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
   316→	}
   317→	return filepath.Join(tmpDirAbs, fmt.Sprintf("pre-prompt-%s.json", sessionID))
   318→}
   319→
   320→// getUntrackedFilesForState returns a list of untracked files using go-git
   321→// Excludes .entire directory
   322→func getUntrackedFilesForState() ([]string, error) {
   323→	repo, err := openRepository()
   324→	if err != nil {
   325→		return nil, err
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   329→	if err != nil {
   330→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   334→	if err != nil {
   335→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	untrackedFiles := []string{}
   339→	for file, st := range status {
   340→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   341→			// Exclude .entire directory
   342→			if !strings.HasPrefix(file, paths.EntireDir+"/") && file != paths.EntireDir {
   343→				untrackedFiles = append(untrackedFiles, file)
   344→			}
   345→		}
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	return untrackedFiles, nil
   349→}

   447→// preTaskStateFile returns the absolute path to the pre-task state file for a tool use.
   448→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   449→func preTaskStateFile(toolUseID string) string {
   450→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
   451→	if err != nil {
   452→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
   453→	}
   454→	return filepath.Join(tmpDirAbs, fmt.Sprintf("pre-task-%s.json", toolUseID))
   455→}
   456→
   457→// preTaskFilePrefix is the prefix for pre-task state files
   458→const preTaskFilePrefix = "pre-task-"
   459→
   460→// FindActivePreTaskFile finds an active pre-task file in .entire/tmp/ and returns
   461→// the parent Task's tool_use_id. Returns ("", false) if no pre-task file exists.
   462→// When multiple pre-task files exist (nested subagents), returns the most recently
   463→// modified one.
   464→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   465→func FindActivePreTaskFile() (taskToolUseID string, found bool) {
   466→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
   467→	if err != nil {
   468→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
   469→	}
   470→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(tmpDirAbs)
   471→	if err != nil {
   472→		return "", false
   473→	}
   474→
   475→	var latestFile string
   476→	var latestTime time.Time
   477→
   478→	for _, entry := range entries {
   479→		if entry.IsDir() {
   480→			continue
   481→		}
   482→		name := entry.Name()
   483→		if !strings.HasPrefix(name, preTaskFilePrefix) || !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   484→			continue
   485→		}
   486→
   487→		// Check modification time for nested subagent handling
   488→		info, err := entry.Info()
   489→		if err != nil {
   490→			continue
   491→		}
   492→		if latestFile == "" || info.ModTime().After(latestTime) {
   493→			latestFile = name
   494→			latestTime = info.ModTime()
   495→		}
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	if latestFile == "" {
   499→		return "", false
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	// Extract tool_use_id from filename: pre-task-<tool_use_id>.json
   503→	toolUseID := strings.TrimPrefix(latestFile, preTaskFilePrefix)
   504→	toolUseID = strings.TrimSuffix(toolUseID, ".json")
   505→	return toolUseID, true
   506→}
   507→
   508→// GetNextCheckpointSequence returns the next sequence number for incremental checkpoints.
   509→// It counts existing checkpoint files in the task metadata checkpoints directory.
   510→// Returns 1 if no checkpoints exist yet.
   511→func GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID string) int {
   512→	// Use the session ID directly as the metadata directory name
   513→	sessionMetadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   514→	taskMetadataDir := strategy.TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, taskToolUseID)
   515→	checkpointsDir := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, "checkpoints")
   516→
   517→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(checkpointsDir)
   518→	if err != nil {
   519→		// Directory doesn't exist or can't be read - start at 1
   520→		return 1
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Count JSON files (checkpoints)
   524→	count := 0
   525→	for _, entry := range entries {
   526→		if !entry.IsDir() && strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   527→			count++
   528→		}
   529→	}
   530→
   531→	return count + 1
   532→}

    18→
    19→// HookHandlerFunc is a function that handles a specific hook event.
    20→type HookHandlerFunc func() error
    21→
    22→// hookRegistry maps (agentName, hookName) to handler functions.
    23→// This allows agents to define their hook vocabulary while keeping
    24→// handler logic in the CLI package (avoiding circular dependencies).
    25→var hookRegistry = map[agent.AgentName]map[string]HookHandlerFunc{}
    26→
    27→// RegisterHookHandler registers a handler for an agent's hook.
    28→func RegisterHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string, handler HookHandlerFunc) {
    29→	if hookRegistry[agentName] == nil {
    30→		hookRegistry[agentName] = make(map[string]HookHandlerFunc)
    31→	}
    32→	hookRegistry[agentName][hookName] = handler
    33→}
    34→
    35→// GetHookHandler returns the handler for an agent's hook, or nil if not found.
    36→func GetHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) HookHandlerFunc {
    37→	if handlers, ok := hookRegistry[agentName]; ok {
    38→		return handlers[hookName]
    39→	}
    40→	return nil
    41→}
    42→
    43→// init registers Claude Code hook handlers.
    44→// Each handler checks if Entire is enabled before executing.
    45→//
    46→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Hook handler registration at startup is the intended pattern

   194→
   195→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
   196→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
   197→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
   198→
   199→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
   200→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
   201→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
   202→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
   203→
   204→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
   205→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
   206→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
   207→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
   208→//
   209→
   210→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
   211→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
   212→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
   216→	if err != nil {
   217→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
   218→	}
   219→	return ag, nil
   220→}
   221→
   222→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
   223→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
   224→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
   225→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   226→		Use:    string(agentName),
   227→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
   228→		Hidden: true,
   229→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   230→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
   231→			return nil
   232→		},
   233→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   234→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
   235→				agentHookLogCleanup()
   236→			}
   237→			return nil
   238→		},
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
   242→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	return cmd
   246→}
   247→
   248→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
   249→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
   250→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
   251→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
   252→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
   253→	switch hookName {
   254→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
   255→		return "subagent"
   256→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
   257→		return "tool"
   258→	default:
   259→		return "agent"
   260→	}
   261→}
   262→
   263→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
   264→// It logs hook invocation at DEBUG level and completion with duration at INFO level.
   265→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
   266→	return &cobra.Command{
   267→		Use:    hookName,
   268→		Hidden: true,
   269→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
   270→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   271→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
   272→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   273→				return nil
   274→			}
   275→
   276→			start := time.Now()
   277→
   278→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   279→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   280→
   281→			// Get strategy name for logging
   282→			strategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase
   283→			strategyName = GetStrategy().Name()
   284→
   285→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   286→
   287→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   288→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   289→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   290→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   291→			)
   292→
   293→			handler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)
   294→			if handler == nil {
   295→				logging.Error(ctx, "no handler registered",
   296→					slog.String("hook", hookName),
   297→					slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   298→				)
   299→				return fmt.Errorf("no handler registered for %s/%s", agentName, hookName)
   300→			}
   301→
   302→			// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it
   303→			// without guessing from directory presence
   304→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   305→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   306→
   307→			hookErr := handler()
   308→
   309→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   310→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   311→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   312→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   313→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   314→			)
   315→
   316→			return hookErr
   317→		},
   318→	}
   319→}
   320→

     1→# Plan: Evolve Agent Interface for Contributor-Ready Plugin Surface
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→The CLI is now open source and receiving contributions. The current agent integration surface area requires contributors to:
     6→1. Implement a 17-method `Agent` interface with unclear required-vs-optional boundaries
     7→2. Write per-agent handler functions in the `cli` package (due to import cycles)
     8→3. Register handlers via stringly-typed `(agentName, hookName)` pairs in `hook_registry.go`
     9→4. Understand implicit mappings from agent-native hooks to lifecycle semantics
    10→5. Duplicate framework logic (state transitions, strategy calls, file detection) in each handler
    11→
    12→This plan redesigns the agent interface around explicit lifecycle events with a generic framework dispatcher, making it compile-time safe and contributor-friendly.
    13→
    14→## Design Rationale
    15→
    16→### The core insight: agents are data providers, not orchestrators
    17→
    18→The current architecture grew organically as we added agents. Each agent's handler file (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`) contains a mix of agent-specific parsing and framework orchestration logic — state transitions, strategy calls, file change detection, context generation. When a contributor wants to add a new agent, they have to understand and reproduce all of that framework machinery, which is both error-prone and creates maintenance burden as the framework evolves.
    19→
    20→The key realization is that agents should be **passive data providers**. They know how to parse their own hook inputs and transcript formats, but they should never drive the checkpoint/step lifecycle themselves. The framework should call *into* the agent for data (transcript bytes, modified file lists, prompts, summaries) and handle all the orchestration — state machine transitions, strategy method calls, file change detection, metadata generation — in one place.
    21→
    22→This inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions" to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods."
    23→
    24→### Separating Events from Actions
    25→
    26→We initially conflated what happens (events) with what the framework does about it (actions). For example, "StepSave" was initially proposed as an event type, but it's actually a framework *response* to a TurnEnd event. Getting this distinction right was critical:
    27→
    28→- **Events** are inputs — things that happen in the outside world. They come from agents (SessionStart, TurnStart, TurnEnd, Compaction, SessionEnd, SubagentStart, SubagentEnd) or from git (GitCommit, PrePush). An agent's only job is to translate its native hooks into these normalized events.
    29→- **Actions** are outputs — what the framework does in response. StepSave, Checkpoint, Condense, PhaseTransition, ResetTranscriptOffset. These are entirely internal to the framework and invisible to agent implementers.
    30→
    31→This means a contributor adding a new agent only needs to think about: "which of my hooks correspond to which lifecycle events?" They never need to understand the action dispatch machinery.
    32→
    33→### ParseHookEvent as the contribution surface
    34→
    35→The most important method in the new interface is `ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)`. This is where 90% of agent-specific work lives. The agent reads its native hook payload from stdin, extracts the session ID, transcript path, and any other relevant data, and returns a normalized `Event` — or `nil` if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    36→
    37→This design handles the "pass-through hook" problem elegantly. Agents like Gemini have hooks (before-tool, after-tool, before-model, etc.) that we acknowledge but don't act on. Rather than requiring dummy handler registrations for each, the agent simply returns `nil` from `ParseHookEvent` and the dispatcher does nothing.
    38→
    39→### Why transcript operations are core, not optional
    40→
    41→We initially considered making transcript reading/chunking/analysis optional interfaces, since not all hypothetical future agents might have accessible transcripts. But transcript data is fundamental to the product — it's what gets stored in checkpoints, what drives the "files modified" detection, and what enables session logs and summaries. An agent that can't provide transcript access can't meaningfully participate in the checkpoint lifecycle. Making these methods core forces contributors to think about transcript access upfront rather than discovering the gap later.
    42→
    43→### Step vs Checkpoint terminology
    44→
    45→The codebase had been using "checkpoint" loosely for two distinct concepts: the temporary per-turn saves on shadow branches and the permanent persisted saves on `entire/checkpoints/v1`. We established precise terminology:
    46→- A **Step** is saved to the shadow branch — it's temporary, per-turn, and gets cleaned up on condensation
    47→- A **Checkpoint** is persisted to `entire/checkpoints/v1` — it's permanent and survives branch operations
    48→
    49→This distinction matters because both happen on TurnEnd (the framework saves a step *and* writes a checkpoint), but they have different lifecycles and storage mechanisms. The strategy interface rename (`SaveChanges` → `SaveStep`, `SaveTaskCheckpoint` → `SaveTaskStep`) makes the code self-documenting.
    50→
    51→### Compaction and session handoff
    52→
    53→Two lifecycle events we identified during design that weren't previously modeled:
    54→
    55→**Compaction** occurs when an agent compresses its context window (e.g., Gemini's pre-compress hook). This triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd but also resets the transcript offset, since the transcript may be truncated or reorganized. We also added defensive detection: if `GetTranscriptPosition()` returns a value smaller than the stored offset, the framework treats it as an implicit compaction even without an explicit event.
    56→
    57→**Session handoff** occurs when Claude starts a new session ID (observed after exiting plan mode). Rather than building complex session-linking machinery now, we added a `PreviousSessionID` field on `Event` for future use. The current shadow branch architecture already handles this gracefully — multiple session IDs naturally share the same shadow branch and condense together.
    58→
    59→### Terminology (established in this plan)
    60→- **Step** = saved to shadow branch (`entire/<hash>-<worktreeHash>`) — temporary, per-turn
    61→- **Checkpoint** = persisted to `entire/checkpoints/v1` — permanent, on turn end and git commit
    62→
    63→### Prerequisites
    64→- **ENT-250** (ActionHandler interface for compile-time exhaustive action dispatch) should be completed first. It's self-contained and establishes the pattern we'll extend here.
    65→
    66→---
    67→
    68→## Phase 1: Define Event Types and New Agent Interface
    69→
    70→### 1a. Event types (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go` — new file)
    71→
    72→```go
    73→type EventType int
    74→const (
    75→    SessionStart  EventType = iota  // Agent session begins
    76→    TurnStart                       // User submitted a prompt
    77→    TurnEnd                         // Agent finished responding
    78→    Compaction                      // Agent about to compress context
    79→    SessionEnd                      // Session terminated
    80→    SubagentStart                   // Subagent spawned
    81→    SubagentEnd                     // Subagent completed
    82→)
    83→
    84→type Event struct {
    85→    Type              EventType
    86→    SessionID         string
    87→    PreviousSessionID string     // Non-empty = continuation/handoff
    88→    SessionRef        string     // Path to transcript file
    89→    Prompt            string     // For TurnStart: user's prompt
    90→    Timestamp         time.Time
    91→
    92→    // Subagent-specific
    93→    ToolUseID         string
    94→    SubagentID        string
    95→    ToolInput         json.RawMessage  // Raw tool input (for subagent type/description extraction)
    96→
    97→    // Response — message to show the user via the agent
    98→    ResponseMessage   string
    99→}
   100→```

     1→// hooks_geminicli_handlers.go contains Gemini CLI specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→	"strings"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// ErrSessionSkipped is returned when a session should be skipped (e.g., due to concurrent warning).
    22→var ErrSessionSkipped = errors.New("session skipped")
    23→
    24→// handleGeminiSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Gemini CLI.
    25→func handleGeminiSessionStart() error {
    26→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
    27→}
    28→
    29→// handleGeminiSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Gemini CLI.
    30→// This fires when the user explicitly exits the session (via "exit" or "logout" commands).
    31→// It parses the session-end input and marks the session as ended so that subsequent
    32→// git hooks (e.g., post-commit) can trigger condensation for ended sessions.
    33→func handleGeminiSessionEnd() error { // Parse stdin once upfront — all subsequent steps use ctx.sessionID
    34→	ctx, err := parseGeminiSessionEnd()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		if errors.Is(err, ErrSessionSkipped) {
    37→			return nil
    38→		}
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session-end input: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Mark session as ended using the parsed session ID
    43→	if err := markSessionEnded(ctx.sessionID); err != nil {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	return nil
    48→}
    49→
    50→// geminiSessionContext holds parsed session data for Gemini commits.
    51→type geminiSessionContext struct {
    52→	sessionID      string
    53→	transcriptPath string
    54→	sessionDir     string
    55→	sessionDirAbs  string
    56→	transcriptData []byte
    57→	allPrompts     []string
    58→	summary        string
    59→	modifiedFiles  []string
    60→	commitMessage  string
    61→}
    62→
    63→// parseGeminiSessionEnd parses the session-end hook input and validates transcript.
    64→func parseGeminiSessionEnd() (*geminiSessionContext, error) {
    65→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    76→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
    77→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
    78→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    79→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    80→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    81→	)
    82→
    83→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    84→	if sessionID == "" {
    85→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
    89→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	return &geminiSessionContext{
    94→		sessionID:      sessionID,
    95→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
    96→	}, nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// setupGeminiSessionDir creates session directory and copies transcript.
   100→func setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   101→	ctx.sessionDir = paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(ctx.sessionID)
   102→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(ctx.sessionDir)
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		sessionDirAbs = ctx.sessionDir
   105→	}
   106→	ctx.sessionDirAbs = sessionDirAbs
   107→
   108→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   113→	if err := copyFile(ctx.transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   114→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   117→
   118→	transcriptData, err := os.ReadFile(ctx.transcriptPath)
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→	ctx.transcriptData = transcriptData
   123→
   124→	return nil
   125→}
   126→
   127→// extractGeminiMetadata extracts prompts, summary, and modified files from transcript.
   128→func extractGeminiMetadata(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   129→	allPrompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts(ctx.transcriptData)
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", err)
   132→	}
   133→	ctx.allPrompts = allPrompts
   134→
   135→	promptFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   136→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   137→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   138→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   141→
   142→	summary, err := geminicli.ExtractLastAssistantMessage(ctx.transcriptData)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", err)
   145→	}
   146→	ctx.summary = summary
   147→
   148→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   149→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   150→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   151→	}
   152→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   153→
   154→	modifiedFiles, err := geminicli.ExtractModifiedFiles(ctx.transcriptData)
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", err)
   157→	}
   158→	ctx.modifiedFiles = modifiedFiles
   159→
   160→	lastPrompt := ""
   161→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   162→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   163→	}
   164→	ctx.commitMessage = generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   165→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", ctx.commitMessage)
   166→
   167→	return nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→// commitGeminiSession commits the session changes using the strategy.
   171→func commitGeminiSession(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   172→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   180→	}
   181→	if preState != nil {
   182→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Loaded pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files, start message index: %d\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles), preState.StartMessageIndex)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state
   186→	var startMessageIndex int
   187→	if preState != nil {
   188→		startMessageIndex = preState.StartMessageIndex
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Calculate token usage for this prompt/response cycle (Gemini-specific)
   192→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   193→	if ctx.transcriptPath != "" {
   194→		usage, tokenErr := geminicli.CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(ctx.transcriptPath, startMessageIndex)
   195→		if tokenErr != nil {
   196→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   197→		} else if usage != nil && usage.APICallCount > 0 {
   198→			tokenUsage = usage
   199→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Token usage for this checkpoint: input=%d, output=%d, cache_read=%d, api_calls=%d\n",
   200→				tokenUsage.InputTokens, tokenUsage.OutputTokens, tokenUsage.CacheReadTokens, tokenUsage.APICallCount)
   201→		}
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   205→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(ctx.modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   211→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   212→	if changes != nil {
   213→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   214→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   218→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   219→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   220→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   221→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   222→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   223→		}
   224→		return nil
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   228→
   229→	contextFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   230→	if err := createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, ctx.commitMessage, ctx.sessionID, ctx.allPrompts, ctx.summary); err != nil {
   231→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   234→
   235→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   236→	if err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	strat := GetStrategy()
   241→
   242→	// Get agent type from the hook agent (determined by which hook command is running)
   243→	// This is authoritative - if we're in "entire hooks gemini session-end", it's Gemini CLI
   244→	hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   245→	if agentErr != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", agentErr)
   247→	}
   248→	agentType := hookAgent.Type()
   249→
   250→	// Get transcript identifier at start from pre-prompt state
   251→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   252→	if preState != nil {
   253→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	saveCtx := strategy.SaveContext{
   257→		SessionID:                ctx.sessionID,
   258→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   259→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   260→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   261→		MetadataDir:              ctx.sessionDir,
   262→		MetadataDirAbs:           ctx.sessionDirAbs,
   263→		CommitMessage:            ctx.commitMessage,
   264→		TranscriptPath:           ctx.transcriptPath,
   265→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   266→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   267→		AgentType:                agentType,
   268→		StepTranscriptStart:      startMessageIndex,
   269→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   270→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(saveCtx); err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   278→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session saved successfully\n")
   282→	return nil
   283→}
   284→
   285→// logFileChanges logs the modified, new, and deleted files to stderr.
   286→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   287→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   288→	for _, file := range modified {
   289→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   290→	}
   291→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   292→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   293→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   294→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   298→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   299→		for _, file := range deleted {
   300→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   301→		}
   302→	}
   303→}
   304→
   305→// createContextFileForGemini creates a context.md file for Gemini sessions.
   306→func createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   307→	var sb strings.Builder
   308→
   309→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   310→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Session ID: %s\n", sessionID))
   311→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   312→
   313→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   314→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   315→		for i, p := range prompts {
   316→			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p))
   317→		}
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	if summary != "" {
   321→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   322→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   323→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   327→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   328→	}
   329→	return nil
   330→}
   331→
   332→// handleGeminiBeforeTool handles the BeforeTool hook for Gemini CLI.
   333→// This is similar to Claude Code's PreToolUse hook but applies to all tools.
   334→func handleGeminiBeforeTool() error {
   335→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   336→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   337→	if err != nil {
   338→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	// Parse hook input
   342→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookPreToolUse, os.Stdin)
   343→	if err != nil {
   344→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   348→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-before-tool",
   349→		slog.String("hook", "before-tool"),
   350→		slog.String("hook_type", "tool"),
   351→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   352→		slog.String("tool_name", input.ToolName),
   353→	)
   354→
   355→	// For now, BeforeTool is mainly for logging and potential future use
   356→	// We don't need to do anything special before tool execution
   357→	return nil
   358→}

   383→	// For now, AfterTool is mainly for logging

   387→
   388→// handleGeminiBeforeAgent handles the BeforeAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   389→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's UserPromptSubmit - it fires when the user submits a prompt.
   390→// We capture the initial state here so we can track what files were modified during the session.
   391→// It also checks for concurrent sessions and blocks if another session has uncommitted changes.
   392→func handleGeminiBeforeAgent() error {
   393→	// Always use the Gemini agent for Gemini hooks (don't use GetAgent() which may
   394→	// return Claude based on auto-detection in environments like VSCode)
   395→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	// Parse hook input - BeforeAgent provides user prompt info similar to UserPromptSubmit
   401→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
   402→	if err != nil {
   403→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   407→
   408→	// Log with prompt if available (Gemini provides the user's prompt in BeforeAgent)
   409→	logArgs := []any{
   410→		slog.String("hook", "before-agent"),
   411→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   412→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   413→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   414→	}
   415→	if input.UserPrompt != "" {
   416→		// Truncate long prompts for logging
   417→		promptPreview := input.UserPrompt
   418→		if len(promptPreview) > 100 {
   419→			promptPreview = promptPreview[:100] + "..."
   420→		}
   421→		logArgs = append(logArgs, slog.String("prompt_preview", promptPreview))
   422→	}
   423→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-before-agent", logArgs...)
   424→
   425→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   426→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Capture pre-prompt state with transcript position (Gemini-specific)
   430→	// This captures both untracked files and the current transcript message count
   431→	// so we can calculate token usage for just this prompt/response cycle
   432→	if err := CaptureGeminiPrePromptState(input.SessionID, input.SessionRef); err != nil {
   433→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-prompt state: %w", err)
   434→	}
   435→
   436→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   437→	strat := GetStrategy()
   438→
   439→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (git hooks, gitignore, metadata branch).
   440→	// Done here at turn start so hooks are installed before any mid-turn commits.
   441→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   442→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   446→		agentType := ag.Type()
   447→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(input.SessionID, agentType, input.SessionRef, input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
   448→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   449→		}
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	return nil
   453→}
   454→
   455→// handleGeminiAfterAgent handles the AfterAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   456→// This fires after the agent has finished processing and generated a response.
   457→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's "Stop" hook - it commits the session changes with metadata.
   458→// AfterAgent fires after EVERY user prompt/response cycle, making it the correct place
   459→// for checkpoint creation (not SessionEnd, which only fires on explicit exit).
   460→func handleGeminiAfterAgent() error {
   461→	// Always use Gemini agent for Gemini hooks
   462→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   463→	if err != nil {
   464→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	// Parse hook input using HookStop - AfterAgent provides the same data as Stop
   468→	// (session_id, transcript_path) which is what we need for committing
   469→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   470→	if err != nil {
   471→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   472→	}
   473→
   474→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   475→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-after-agent",
   476→		slog.String("hook", "after-agent"),
   477→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   478→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   479→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   480→	)
   481→
   482→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   483→	if sessionID == "" {
   484→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   488→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   489→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   493→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   494→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   495→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	// Create session context and commit
   499→	ctx := &geminiSessionContext{
   500→		sessionID:      sessionID,
   501→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	if err := setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx); err != nil {
   505→		return err
   506→	}
   507→
   508→	if err := extractGeminiMetadata(ctx); err != nil {
   509→		return err
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	if err := commitGeminiSession(ctx); err != nil {
   513→		return err
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	// Transition session ACTIVE → IDLE (equivalent to Claude's transitionSessionTurnEnd)
   517→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   518→
   519→	return nil
   520→}
   521→
   522→// handleGeminiBeforeModel handles the BeforeModel hook for Gemini CLI.

   531→

   536→	}
   537→
   538→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   539→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-before-model",
   540→		slog.String("hook", "before-model"),
   541→		slog.String("hook_type", "model"),
   542→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   543→	)
   544→
   545→	// For now, BeforeModel is mainly for logging
   546→	// Future: Could be used for request interception/modification
   547→	return nil
   548→}

   605→// handleGeminiPreCompress handles the PreCompress hook for Gemini CLI.
   606→// This fires before chat history compression - useful for backing up transcript.
   607→func handleGeminiPreCompress() error {
   608→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   609→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   610→	if err != nil {
   611→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   612→	}
   613→
   614→	// Parse hook input
   615→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   616→	if err != nil {
   617→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   618→	}
   619→
   620→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   621→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-pre-compress",
   622→		slog.String("hook", "pre-compress"),
   623→		slog.String("hook_type", "session"),
   624→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   625→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   626→	)
   627→
   628→	// PreCompress is important for ensuring we capture the transcript before compression
   629→	// The transcript_path gives us access to the full conversation before it's compressed
   630→	// Future: Could automatically backup/checkpoint the transcript here
   631→	return nil
   632→}
   633→
   634→// handleGeminiNotification handles the Notification hook for Gemini CLI.
   635→// This fires on notification events (errors, warnings, info).
   636→func handleGeminiNotification() error {
   637→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   638→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   639→	if err != nil {
   640→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	// Parse hook input
   644→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   645→	if err != nil {
   646→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   647→	}
   648→
   649→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   650→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-notification",
   651→		slog.String("hook", "notification"),
   652→		slog.String("hook_type", "notification"),
   653→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   654→	)
   655→
   656→	// For now, Notification is mainly for logging
   657→	// Future: Could be used for error tracking/alerting
   658→	return nil
   659→}

    19→// PrePromptState stores the state captured before a user prompt
    20→type PrePromptState struct {
    21→	SessionID      string   `json:"session_id"`
    22→	Timestamp      string   `json:"timestamp"`
    23→	UntrackedFiles []string `json:"untracked_files"`
    24→
    25→	// StartMessageIndex is the message count in the transcript when this state
    26→	// was captured. Used for calculating token usage since the prompt started.
    27→	// Only set for Gemini sessions. Zero means not set or session just started.
    28→	StartMessageIndex int `json:"start_message_index,omitempty"`
    29→
    30→	// Transcript position at prompt start - tracks what was added during this step/turn.
    31→	// Used for Claude Code sessions.
    32→	LastTranscriptIdentifier string `json:"last_transcript_identifier,omitempty"` // Last identifier when prompt started (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini)
    33→	StepTranscriptStart      int    `json:"step_transcript_start,omitempty"`      // Transcript line count when this step/turn started
    34→
    35→	// Deprecated: LastTranscriptLineCount is the old name for StepTranscriptStart.
    36→	// Kept for backward compatibility when reading state files written by older CLI versions.
    37→	LastTranscriptLineCount int `json:"last_transcript_line_count,omitempty"`
    38→}

    40→// PreUntrackedFiles returns the untracked files list, or nil if the receiver is nil.
    41→// This nil-vs-empty distinction lets DetectFileChanges know whether to skip new-file detection.
    42→// When the receiver is non-nil but UntrackedFiles is nil (e.g., old state files deserialized with
    43→// "untracked_files": null), returns an empty non-nil slice so that all current untracked files
    44→// are correctly treated as new.
    45→func (s *PrePromptState) PreUntrackedFiles() []string {
    46→	if s == nil {
    47→		return nil
    48→	}
    49→	if s.UntrackedFiles == nil {
    50→		return []string{}
    51→	}
    52→	return s.UntrackedFiles
    53→}
    54→
    55→// normalizePrePromptState migrates deprecated fields after loading from JSON.
    56→func (s *PrePromptState) normalizePrePromptState() {
    57→	if s.StepTranscriptStart == 0 && s.LastTranscriptLineCount > 0 {
    58→		s.StepTranscriptStart = s.LastTranscriptLineCount
    59→	}

    63→// CapturePrePromptState captures current untracked files and transcript position before a prompt
    64→// and saves them to a state file.
    65→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    66→// The transcriptPath parameter is optional - if empty, transcript position won't be captured.
    67→func CapturePrePromptState(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
    68→	if sessionID == "" {
    69→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Get absolute path for tmp directory
    73→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// Create tmp directory if it doesn't exist
    79→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
    80→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create tmp directory: %w", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Get list of untracked files (excluding .entire directory itself)
    84→	untrackedFiles, err := getUntrackedFilesForState()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get untracked files: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	// Get transcript position (last UUID and line count)
    90→	var transcriptPos TranscriptPosition
    91→	if transcriptPath != "" {
    92→		transcriptPos, err = GetTranscriptPosition(transcriptPath)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			// Log warning but don't fail - transcript position is optional
    95→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get transcript position: %v\n", err)
    96→		}
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Create state file
   100→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   101→	state := PrePromptState{
   102→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   103→		Timestamp:                time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   104→		UntrackedFiles:           untrackedFiles,
   105→		LastTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptPos.LastUUID,
   106→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptPos.LineCount,
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal state: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   115→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write state file: %w", err)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Captured state before prompt: %d untracked files, transcript at line %d (uuid: %s)\n",
   119→		len(untrackedFiles), transcriptPos.LineCount, transcriptPos.LastUUID)
   120→	return nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→// CaptureGeminiPrePromptState captures current untracked files and transcript position
   124→// before a prompt for Gemini sessions. This is called by the BeforeAgent hook.
   125→// The transcriptPath is the path to the Gemini session transcript (JSON format).
   126→func CaptureGeminiPrePromptState(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
   127→	if sessionID == "" {
   128→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	// Get absolute path for tmp directory
   132→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
   133→	if err != nil {
   134→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Create tmp directory if it doesn't exist
   138→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   139→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create tmp directory: %w", err)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// Get list of untracked files (excluding .entire directory itself)
   143→	untrackedFiles, err := getUntrackedFilesForState()
   144→	if err != nil {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get untracked files: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Get transcript position (message count and last message ID) for Gemini
   149→	var startMessageIndex int
   150→	var lastMessageID string
   151→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   152→		// Read transcript and extract both message count and last message ID
   153→		if data, readErr := os.ReadFile(transcriptPath); readErr == nil && len(data) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   154→			var transcript struct {
   155→				Messages []struct {
   156→					ID string `json:"id"`
   157→				} `json:"messages"`
   158→			}
   159→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); jsonErr == nil {
   160→				startMessageIndex = len(transcript.Messages)
   161→				if startMessageIndex > 0 {
   162→					lastMessageID = transcript.Messages[startMessageIndex-1].ID
   163→				}
   164→			} else {
   165→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript for message tracking: %v\n", jsonErr)
   166→			}
   167→		}
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// Create state file
   171→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   172→	state := PrePromptState{
   173→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   174→		Timestamp:                time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   175→		UntrackedFiles:           untrackedFiles,
   176→		StartMessageIndex:        startMessageIndex,
   177→		LastTranscriptIdentifier: lastMessageID,
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   181→	if err != nil {
   182→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal state: %w", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write state file: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Captured Gemini state before prompt: %d untracked files, transcript position: %d (last msg id: %s)\n", len(untrackedFiles), startMessageIndex, lastMessageID)
   190→	return nil
   191→}

   193→// LoadPrePromptState loads previously captured state.

   195→func LoadPrePromptState(sessionID string) (*PrePromptState, error) {
   196→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   197→
   198→	if !fileExists(stateFile) {

   201→
   202→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
   203→	if err != nil {
