   591→	// returns all untracked files as New and also returns Modified files.
   592→
   593→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()

   603→	trackedFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "tracked.txt")

The bug is on line 365-368: when `ReadBytes('\n')` returns EOF with data (a final line without trailing newline), the data is discarded without incrementing `lineCount`.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01JmBfcAvfSRP1ES8Q9hUds2
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "\tfor {\n\t\t_, err := reader.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tlineCount++\n\t}",
  "new_string": "\tfor {\n\t\tline, err := reader.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\tif len(line) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tlineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tlineCount++\n\t}"

     1→# Plan: Evolve Agent Interface for Contributor-Ready Plugin Surface
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→The CLI is now open source and receiving contributions. The current agent integration surface area requires contributors to:
     6→1. Implement a 17-method `Agent` interface with unclear required-vs-optional boundaries
     7→2. Write per-agent handler functions in the `cli` package (due to import cycles)
     8→3. Register handlers via stringly-typed `(agentName, hookName)` pairs in `hook_registry.go`
     9→4. Understand implicit mappings from agent-native hooks to lifecycle semantics
    10→5. Duplicate framework logic (state transitions, strategy calls, file detection) in each handler
    11→
    12→This plan redesigns the agent interface around explicit lifecycle events with a generic framework dispatcher, making it compile-time safe and contributor-friendly.
    13→
    14→## Design Rationale
    15→
    16→### The core insight: agents are data providers, not orchestrators
    17→
    18→The current architecture grew organically as we added agents. Each agent's handler file (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`, `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`) contains a mix of agent-specific parsing and framework orchestration logic — state transitions, strategy calls, file change detection, context generation. When a contributor wants to add a new agent, they have to understand and reproduce all of that framework machinery, which is both error-prone and creates maintenance burden as the framework evolves.
    19→
    20→The key realization is that agents should be **passive data providers**. They know how to parse their own hook inputs and transcript formats, but they should never drive the checkpoint/step lifecycle themselves. The framework should call *into* the agent for data (transcript bytes, modified file lists, prompts, summaries) and handle all the orchestration — state machine transitions, strategy method calls, file change detection, metadata generation — in one place.
    21→
    22→This inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions" to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods."
    23→
    24→### Separating Events from Actions
    25→
    26→We initially conflated what happens (events) with what the framework does about it (actions). For example, "StepSave" was initially proposed as an event type, but it's actually a framework *response* to a TurnEnd event. Getting this distinction right was critical:
    27→
    28→- **Events** are inputs — things that happen in the outside world. They come from agents (SessionStart, TurnStart, TurnEnd, Compaction, SessionEnd, SubagentStart, SubagentEnd) or from git (GitCommit, PrePush). An agent's only job is to translate its native hooks into these normalized events.
    29→- **Actions** are outputs — what the framework does in response. StepSave, Checkpoint, Condense, PhaseTransition, ResetTranscriptOffset. These are entirely internal to the framework and invisible to agent implementers.
    30→
    31→This means a contributor adding a new agent only needs to think about: "which of my hooks correspond to which lifecycle events?" They never need to understand the action dispatch machinery.
    32→
    33→### ParseHookEvent as the contribution surface
    34→
    35→The most important method in the new interface is `ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)`. This is where 90% of agent-specific work lives. The agent reads its native hook payload from stdin, extracts the session ID, transcript path, and any other relevant data, and returns a normalized `Event` — or `nil` if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    36→
    37→This design handles the "pass-through hook" problem elegantly. Agents like Gemini have hooks (before-tool, after-tool, before-model, etc.) that we acknowledge but don't act on. Rather than requiring dummy handler registrations for each, the agent simply returns `nil` from `ParseHookEvent` and the dispatcher does nothing.
    38→
    39→### Why transcript operations are core, not optional
    40→
    41→We initially considered making transcript reading/chunking/analysis optional interfaces, since not all hypothetical future agents might have accessible transcripts. But transcript data is fundamental to the product — it's what gets stored in checkpoints, what drives the "files modified" detection, and what enables session logs and summaries. An agent that can't provide transcript access can't meaningfully participate in the checkpoint lifecycle. Making these methods core forces contributors to think about transcript access upfront rather than discovering the gap later.
    42→
    43→### Step vs Checkpoint terminology
    44→
    45→The codebase had been using "checkpoint" loosely for two distinct concepts: the temporary per-turn saves on shadow branches and the permanent persisted saves on `entire/checkpoints/v1`. We established precise terminology:
    46→- A **Step** is saved to the shadow branch — it's temporary, per-turn, and gets cleaned up on condensation
    47→- A **Checkpoint** is persisted to `entire/checkpoints/v1` — it's permanent and survives branch operations
    48→
    49→This distinction matters because both happen on TurnEnd (the framework saves a step *and* writes a checkpoint), but they have different lifecycles and storage mechanisms. The strategy interface rename (`SaveChanges` → `SaveStep`, `SaveTaskCheckpoint` → `SaveTaskStep`) makes the code self-documenting.
    50→
    51→### Compaction and session handoff
    52→
    53→Two lifecycle events we identified during design that weren't previously modeled:
    54→
    55→**Compaction** occurs when an agent compresses its context window (e.g., Gemini's pre-compress hook). This triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd but also resets the transcript offset, since the transcript may be truncated or reorganized. We also added defensive detection: if `GetTranscriptPosition()` returns a value smaller than the stored offset, the framework treats it as an implicit compaction even without an explicit event.
    56→
    57→**Session handoff** occurs when Claude starts a new session ID (observed after exiting plan mode). Rather than building complex session-linking machinery now, we added a `PreviousSessionID` field on `Event` for future use. The current shadow branch architecture already handles this gracefully — multiple session IDs naturally share the same shadow branch and condense together.
    58→
    59→### Terminology (established in this plan)
    60→- **Step** = saved to shadow branch (`entire/<hash>-<worktreeHash>`) — temporary, per-turn
    61→- **Checkpoint** = persisted to `entire/checkpoints/v1` — permanent, on turn end and git commit
    62→
    63→### Prerequisites
    64→- **ENT-250** (ActionHandler interface for compile-time exhaustive action dispatch) should be completed first. It's self-contained and establishes the pattern we'll extend here.
    65→
    66→---
    67→
    68→## Phase 1: Define Event Types and New Agent Interface
    69→
    70→### 1a. Event types (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go` — new file)
    71→
    72→```go
    73→type EventType int
    74→const (
    75→    SessionStart  EventType = iota  // Agent session begins
    76→    TurnStart                       // User submitted a prompt
    77→    TurnEnd                         // Agent finished responding
    78→    Compaction                      // Agent about to compress context
    79→    SessionEnd                      // Session terminated
    80→    SubagentStart                   // Subagent spawned
    81→    SubagentEnd                     // Subagent completed
    82→)
    83→
    84→type Event struct {
    85→    Type              EventType
    86→    SessionID         string
    87→    PreviousSessionID string     // Non-empty = continuation/handoff
    88→    SessionRef        string     // Path to transcript file
    89→    Prompt            string     // For TurnStart: user's prompt
    90→    Timestamp         time.Time
    91→
    92→    // Subagent-specific
    93→    ToolUseID         string
    94→    SubagentID        string
    95→    ToolInput         json.RawMessage  // Raw tool input (for subagent type/description extraction)
    96→
    97→    // Response — message to show the user via the agent
    98→    ResponseMessage   string
    99→}
   100→```
   101→
   102→### 1b. New `Agent` interface (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go` — modify)
   103→
   104→Replace the current 17-method interface with a 14-method interface in three clear groups:
   105→
   106→```go
   107→type Agent interface {
   108→    // --- Identity (5 methods, all trivial one-liners) ---
   109→    Name() AgentName
   110→    Type() AgentType
   111→    Description() string
   112→    DetectPresence() (bool, error)
   113→    ProtectedDirs() []string
   114→
   115→    // --- Event Mapping (2 methods, the core contribution surface) ---
   116→    HookNames() []string
   117→    ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
   118→
   119→    // --- Transcript Operations (7 methods, called by framework during actions) ---
   120→    ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
   121→    GetTranscriptPosition(sessionRef string) (int, error)
   122→    ExtractModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (files []string, newOffset int, err error)
   123→    ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   124→    ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   125→    ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
   126→    ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
   127→}
   128→```
   129→
   130→Methods removed from core interface (moved to optional interfaces or framework):
   131→- `GetHookConfigPath()` → move to `HookInstaller`
   132→- `SupportsHooks()` → inferred from `HookInstaller` implementation
   133→- `ParseHookInput()` → replaced by `ParseHookEvent()`
   134→- `GetSessionID()` → extracted inside `ParseHookEvent()`
   135→- `TransformSessionID()` / `ExtractAgentSessionID()` → internal to agent, used inside `ParseHookEvent()`
   136→- `GetSessionDir()` / `ResolveSessionFile()` / `ReadSession()` / `WriteSession()` / `FormatResumeCommand()` → move to `SessionResumer`
   137→
   138→### 1c. Optional interfaces (keep existing, reorganize)
   139→
   140→```go
   141→// For agents where we can auto-install hooks
   142→type HookInstaller interface {
   143→    Agent
   144→    GetHookConfigPath() string
   145→    InstallHooks(localDev, force bool) (int, error)
   146→    UninstallHooks() error
   147→    AreHooksInstalled() bool
   148→    GetSupportedHooks() []HookType  // Keep for setup validation
   149→}
   150→
   151→// For agents that support session resumption (rewind --resume)
   152→type SessionResumer interface {
   153→    Agent
   154→    GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
   155→    ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
   156→    ReadSession(sessionRef string) (*AgentSession, error)
   157→    WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
   158→    FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
   159→}
   160→
   161→// For agents without hooks — file-watching based
   162→type FileWatcher interface {
   163→    Agent
   164→    WatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   165→    OnFileChange(path string) (*Event, error)
   166→}
   167→```
   168→
   169→Remove `TranscriptAnalyzer` and `TranscriptChunker` as separate optional interfaces — their methods are now in the core `Agent` interface.
   170→
   171→### 1d. Agent-specific concerns
   172→
   173→**Transcript flush (Claude-specific):** Add an optional interface:
   174→```go
   175→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   176→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   177→type TranscriptPreparer interface {
   178→    Agent
   179→    PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error
   180→}
   181→```
   182→The framework calls this before `ReadTranscript()` if implemented. Claude implements it with the sentinel polling logic from `waitForTranscriptFlush()`.
   183→
   184→**Token usage calculation:** Add an optional interface:
   185→```go
   186→// TokenCalculator provides token usage for a session.
   187→type TokenCalculator interface {
   188→    Agent
   189→    CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)
   190→}
   191→```
   192→Both agents implement this with their format-specific logic. The framework calls it during StepSave/Checkpoint if available.
   193→
   194→### Files to modify/create:
   195→- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go` — **new**: Event types
   196→- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go` — **modify**: new interface, keep old as `LegacyAgent` temporarily
   197→- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go` — **modify**: implement new interface
   198→- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` — **modify**: implement new interface
   199→
   200→---
   201→
   202→## Phase 2: Build Generic Lifecycle Dispatcher
   203→
   204→### 2a. Pre-prompt state unification
   205→
   206→Currently there are two separate functions: `CapturePrePromptState()` (Claude, JSONL line counting) and `CaptureGeminiPrePromptState()` (Gemini, JSON message counting). Both capture untracked files + transcript position.
   207→
   208→Unify into a single function that uses the Agent interface:
   209→```go
   210→func CapturePrePromptState(ag agent.Agent, sessionID, sessionRef string) error {
   211→    position, err := ag.GetTranscriptPosition(sessionRef)
   212→    // ... store position + untracked files
   213→}
   214→```
   215→
   216→The `PrePromptState` struct already has both `StepTranscriptStart` (used by Claude) and `StartMessageIndex` (used by Gemini) — unify these into a single `TranscriptOffset int` field.
   217→
   218→### 2b. Generic dispatcher (`cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — new file)
   219→
   220→Single entry point replacing all per-agent handler logic:
   221→
   222→```go
   223→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   224→    switch event.Type {
   225→    case agent.SessionStart:
   226→        return handleSessionStart(ag, event)
   227→    case agent.TurnStart:
   228→        return handleTurnStart(ag, event)
   229→    case agent.TurnEnd:
   230→        return handleTurnEnd(ag, event)
   231→    case agent.Compaction:
   232→        return handleCompaction(ag, event)
   233→    case agent.SessionEnd:
   234→        return handleSessionEnd(ag, event)
   235→    case agent.SubagentStart:
   236→        return handleSubagentStart(ag, event)
   237→    case agent.SubagentEnd:
   238→        return handleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
   239→    }
   240→}
   241→```
   242→
   243→Each handler function encapsulates the framework logic that's currently duplicated across `commitWithMetadata()` and `handleGeminiAfterAgent()`:
   244→
   245→**`handleTurnEnd(ag, event)`** — the main flow (framework orchestrates, agent provides data):
   246→1. Validate transcript exists
   247→2. If agent implements `TranscriptPreparer`, call `ag.PrepareTranscript()` *(Claude: flush wait)*
   248→3. Create session metadata directory
   249→4. Read transcript via `ag.ReadTranscript(event.SessionRef)` *(agent provides bytes)*
   250→5. Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   251→6. Extract modified files via `ag.ExtractModifiedFiles(sessionRef, offset)` *(agent parses its format)*
   252→7. Extract prompts via `ag.ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, offset)` *(agent parses its format)*
   253→8. Extract summary via `ag.ExtractSummary(sessionRef)` *(agent parses its format)*
   254→9. Detect file changes via `DetectFileChanges()` *(framework, git status)*
   255→10. Filter and normalize paths *(framework)*
   256→11. If no changes: transition phase, clean up, return
   257→12. Create context file *(framework, agent-agnostic)*
   258→13. If agent implements `TokenCalculator`, call `ag.CalculateTokenUsage()` *(optional)*
   259→14. Build `strategy.StepContext`, call `strat.SaveStep()` — **StepSave action**
   260→15. Persist checkpoint to `entire/checkpoints/v1` — **Checkpoint action**
   261→16. Transition session phase (TurnEnd) *(framework, state machine)*
   262→17. Clean up pre-prompt state *(framework)*
   263→
   264→**`handleTurnStart(ag, event)`:**
   265→1. Capture pre-prompt state using `ag.GetTranscriptPosition()`
   266→2. If strategy implements `SessionInitializer`, call `InitializeSession()`
   267→
   268→**`handleSessionStart(ag, event)`:**
   269→1. Check concurrent sessions
   270→2. Output hook response message
   271→3. Fire `EventSessionStart` on state machine
   272→
   273→**`handleSessionEnd(ag, event)`:**
   274→1. Mark session ended via `markSessionEnded()`
   275→
   276→**`handleCompaction(ag, event)`:**
   277→1. Same as `handleTurnEnd()` but also resets transcript offset in session state
   278→
   279→**`handleSubagentStart(ag, event)`:**
   280→1. Capture pre-task state
   281→
   282→**`handleSubagentEnd(ag, event)`:**
   283→1. Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   284→2. Detect file changes
   285→3. Build `TaskStepContext`, call `strat.SaveTaskStep()`
   286→4. Clean up pre-task state
   287→
   288→### 2c. Hook command integration
   289→
   290→Modify `newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()` in `hook_registry.go` to use the generic dispatcher:
   291→
   292→```go
   293→RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   294→    // ... existing setup (repo check, logging, agent resolution) ...
   295→
   296→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, os.Stdin)
   297→    if err != nil {
   298→        return err
   299→    }
   300→    if event == nil {
   301→        return nil  // Agent says: no lifecycle action needed
   302→    }
   303→    return DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   304→}
   305→```
   306→
   307→This replaces the entire `hookRegistry` map + `GetHookHandler()` lookup + per-agent `init()` registration.
   308→
   309→### Files to modify/create:
   310→- `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — **new**: generic dispatcher
   311→- `cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go` — **modify**: simplify to use dispatcher
   312→- `cmd/entire/cli/state.go` — **modify**: unify `CapturePrePromptState` / `CaptureGeminiPrePromptState`
   313→
   314→---
   315→
   316→## Phase 3: Migrate Agents
   317→
   318→### 3a. Migrate Claude Code
   319→
   320→Implement `ParseHookEvent()` on `ClaudeCodeAgent`:
   321→
   322→```go
   323→func (a *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error) {
   324→    switch hookName {
   325→    case HookNameSessionStart:
   326→        return parseClaudeSessionStart(stdin)
   327→    case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
   328→        return parseClaudeTurnStart(stdin)
   329→    case HookNameStop:
   330→        return parseClaudeTurnEnd(stdin)
   331→    case HookNameSessionEnd:
   332→        return parseClaudeSessionEnd(stdin)
   333→    case HookNamePreTask:
   334→        return parseClaudeSubagentStart(stdin)
   335→    case HookNamePostTask:
   336→        return parseClaudeSubagentEnd(stdin)
   337→    case HookNamePostTodo:
   338→        return nil, nil  // Agent-specific: handled outside generic dispatcher
   339→    }
   340→    return nil, nil
   341→}
   342→```
   343→
   344→Move transcript methods to satisfy new interface:
   345→- `GetTranscriptPosition()` — already exists on `ClaudeCodeAgent`
   346→- `ExtractModifiedFiles()` — already exists, adjust signature
   347→- `ReadTranscript()` — wrap existing file read
   348→- `ExtractPrompts()` / `ExtractSummary()` — wrap existing transcript parsing functions
   349→- `ChunkTranscript()` / `ReassembleTranscript()` — already exist
   350→
   351→Implement `TranscriptPreparer` for the flush-wait logic.
   352→Implement `TokenCalculator` wrapping `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()`.
   353→
   354→### 3b. Migrate Gemini CLI
   355→
   356→Implement `ParseHookEvent()` on `GeminiCLIAgent`:
   357→
   358→```go
   359→func (a *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error) {
   360→    switch hookName {
   361→    case HookNameSessionStart:
   362→        return parseGeminiSessionStart(stdin)
   363→    case HookNameBeforeAgent:
   364→        return parseGeminiTurnStart(stdin)
   365→    case HookNameAfterAgent:
   366→        return parseGeminiTurnEnd(stdin)
   367→    case HookNameSessionEnd:
   368→        return parseGeminiSessionEnd(stdin)
   369→    case HookNamePreCompress:
   370→        return parseGeminiCompaction(stdin)
   371→    case HookNameBeforeTool, HookNameAfterTool, HookNameBeforeModel,
   372→         HookNameAfterModel, HookNameBeforeToolSelection, HookNameNotification:
   373→        return nil, nil  // Acknowledged, no lifecycle action
   374→    }
   375→    return nil, nil
   376→}
   377→```
   378→
   379→### Files to modify:
   380→- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go` — **modify**: add `ParseHookEvent`, `ReadTranscript`, `PrepareTranscript`, etc.
   381→- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` — **modify**: add `ParseHookEvent`, `ReadTranscript`, etc.
   382→
   383→---
   384→
   385→## Phase 4: Clean Up and Rename
   386→
   387→### 4a. Remove old code
   388→- **Delete** `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`
   389→- **Delete** `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`
   390→- **Remove** `hookRegistry` map and string-based registration from `hook_registry.go`
   391→- **Remove** `HookHandlerFunc` type
   392→- **Remove** the `init()` that registers 20+ handlers
   393→- **Remove** `CaptureGeminiPrePromptState()` (merged into unified version)
   394→- **Remove** old `Agent` interface methods that moved to optional interfaces
   395→
   396→### 4b. Update state machine events
   397→
   398→Add `Compaction` to session phase events (`cmd/entire/cli/session/phase.go`):
   399→- New event: `EventCompaction`
   400→- Transition: from any active phase, save + reset offset, no phase change
   401→- Framework defensively detects compaction: if `GetTranscriptPosition()` returns value < stored offset, treat as compaction
   402→
   403→### 4c. Step/Checkpoint terminology rename
   404→
   405→Rename strategy interface methods to align with Step/Checkpoint terminology:
   406→
   407→| Current | New | Notes |
   408→|---------|-----|-------|
   409→| `SaveChanges(SaveContext)` | `SaveStep(StepContext)` | Core strategy method |
   410→| `SaveTaskCheckpoint(TaskCheckpointContext)` | `SaveTaskStep(TaskStepContext)` | Core strategy method |
   411→| `SaveContext` struct | `StepContext` | Rename type |
   412→| `TaskCheckpointContext` struct | `TaskStepContext` | Rename type |
   413→| `GetRewindPoints()` | Keep as-is | Rewind works from both steps and checkpoints |
   414→| `checkpoint.WriteTemporary()` | Keep as-is | Already semantically "step write" |
   415→| `checkpoint.WriteCommitted()` | Keep as-is | Already semantically "checkpoint write" |
   416→
   417→This rename propagates through:
   418→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go` — interface definition, `StepContext`/`TaskStepContext` structs
   419→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit.go` — `SaveStep()` implementation
   420→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go` — `SaveStep()` implementation
   421→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — uses `SaveContext` → `StepContext`
   422→- `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — builds `StepContext` instead of `SaveContext`
   423→- All test files referencing these types/methods
   424→- CLAUDE.md documentation
   425→
   426→### 4d. PostTodo pass-through
   427→
   428→Claude Code's PostTodo hook stays as a **direct handler** outside the generic dispatcher. In `hook_registry.go`, register it as a special case:
   429→
   430→```go
   431→// PostTodo is Claude-specific: incremental subagent checkpoints.
   432→// Not generalized to the lifecycle dispatcher until other agents need it.
   433→```
   434→
   435→The dispatcher handles the `nil` return from `ParseHookEvent("post-todo", ...)` by doing nothing. The actual PostTodo logic remains in a Claude-specific handler function (moved from `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` to a smaller `hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go` file).
   436→

   451→
   452→## Phase 5: Documentation and Contributor Guide
   453→
   454→- Update CLAUDE.md with new agent interface documentation
   455→- Add `cmd/entire/cli/agent/CONTRIBUTING.md` with step-by-step guide for adding a new agent
   456→- Add compile-time interface assertions in each agent package
   457→
   458→---
   459→
   460→## Migration Strategy
   461→
   462→Phases 1-2 are additive — the new interface and dispatcher can coexist with the old handler system. During Phase 3, we migrate one agent at a time:
   463→
   464→1. Add `ParseHookEvent()` to Claude Code agent, update dispatcher to use it
   465→2. Verify all Claude Code tests pass
   466→3. Delete `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`
   467→4. Repeat for Gemini CLI
   468→5. Delete old registry code
   469→
   470→This ensures we never have a broken intermediate state.
   471→
   472→---
   473→
   474→## Verification
   475→
   476→After each phase:
   477→```bash
   478→mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
   479→```
   480→
   481→End-to-end validation:
   482→1. `entire enable --agent claude-code` → hooks install correctly
   483→2. Start a Claude Code session → SessionStart event dispatched, banner shown
   484→3. Submit a prompt → TurnStart captures pre-prompt state
   485→4. Agent completes → TurnEnd saves step + checkpoint
   486→5. `git commit` → condensation works
   487→6. `entire rewind` → rewind points listed correctly
   488→7. Repeat steps 1-6 for Gemini CLI
   489→
   490→Integration tests in `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/` cover the strategy-level flows. Add new integration tests for the lifecycle dispatcher.
   491→
   492→---
   493→
   494→## Addendum: Validation Against Third-Party Agent Contributions
   495→
   496→We reviewed open PRs for four third-party agents (OpenCode, Codex CLI, Pi, Cursor) and cross-referenced each against official documentation and source code. This section documents what we found and what adjustments the plan needs.
   497→
   498→### Validation confirms the core design
   499→
   500→The strongest signal from the PR review is the **massive duplication of framework logic**. Every contributed agent re-implements the same ~300-line handler pattern: pre-prompt state capture, transcript copying, file change detection, path normalization, context file creation, `strategy.SaveChanges()`, session state transitions. PR #257 (Codex + OpenCode) recognized this independently and extracted a shared `commitAgentSession()` function — which is essentially a partial version of Phase 2's `DispatchLifecycleEvent`. The plan's inversion ("framework calls agent for data") would eliminate ~1500 lines of duplicated handler code across these agents.
   501→
   502→The 7 event types (SessionStart, TurnStart, TurnEnd, Compaction, SessionEnd, SubagentStart, SubagentEnd) are sufficient for all four agents. No agent needs events outside this set. Agents with fewer hooks (e.g., Codex with only TurnEnd today) naturally get degraded functionality — no pre-prompt state capture means git-status-only file detection — which is an acceptable trade-off.
   503→
   504→### Adjustment 1: Framework-level hook input normalization
   505→
   506→**Problem**: Codex CLI delivers its `notify` payload as the **last argv element**, not via stdin. The Rust source (`codex-rs/hooks/src/user_notification.rs`) explicitly sets `stdin(Stdio::null())` and appends JSON via `command.arg(notify_payload)`. PR #337 assumes stdin delivery and would silently fail. PR #257 discovered this and added `GetCurrentHookArgs()` infrastructure.
   507→
   508→**Note**: The Codex team has confirmed that proper hook support (with stdin-based payload delivery and richer lifecycle events beyond just `agent-turn-complete`) is in active development. Once available, this normalization becomes unnecessary for Codex. However, other future agents may have similar delivery mechanisms.
   509→
   510→**Adjustment**: Add input normalization in `newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()` before calling `ParseHookEvent`. If stdin is empty and positional args contain a JSON payload, wrap the last arg into a `bytes.Reader`. This keeps the `Agent` interface clean (`ParseHookEvent` always receives an `io.Reader`) while handling delivery variation at the framework layer:
   511→
   512→```go
   513→RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   514→    // ... existing setup ...
   515→
   516→    input := normalizeHookInput(os.Stdin, args)
   517→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, input)
   518→    // ...
   519→}
   520→
   521→// normalizeHookInput handles agents that deliver payloads via argv instead of stdin.
   522→func normalizeHookInput(stdin io.Reader, args []string) io.Reader {
   523→    // Check if stdin has data
   524→    if f, ok := stdin.(*os.File); ok {
   525→        if stat, err := f.Stat(); err == nil && stat.Size() > 0 {
   526→            return stdin
   527→        }
   528→    }
   529→    // Fall back to last positional arg if it looks like JSON
   530→    if len(args) > 0 {
   531→        last := args[len(args)-1]
   532→        if len(last) > 0 && (last[0] == '{' || last[0] == '[') {
   533→            return strings.NewReader(last)
   534→        }
   535→    }
   536→    return stdin
   537→}
   538→```
   539→
   540→### Adjustment 2: Transcript analysis methods — core vs optional
   541→
   542→**Problem**: The plan states "An agent that can't provide transcript access can't meaningfully participate in the checkpoint lifecycle" and makes all 7 transcript methods core. Evidence from PRs and official docs shows this is *almost* true but not quite:
   543→
   544→- **Cursor** passes `transcript_path` in every hook payload (confirmed in [official docs](https://cursor.com/docs/agent/hooks)), but the transcript file format is not publicly documented. Implementers would need to stub the analysis methods until the format is known.
   545→- **OpenCode** (PR #315, #257) treats transcripts as opaque and relies entirely on git status — yet still provides useful checkpoint functionality.
   546→- **Codex** has full structured transcripts (JSONL RolloutItems) but is transitioning its hook system, meaning the transcript path delivery mechanism may change.
   547→
   548→All agents can provide `ReadTranscript` (raw bytes), `ChunkTranscript`, and `ReassembleTranscript` (storage operations). The divergence is in the 4 analysis methods that require format-specific parsing.
   549→
   550→**Adjustment**: Split transcript operations into core (3 methods) and optional (4 methods):
   551→
   552→```go
   553→type Agent interface {
   554→    // ... Identity (5) + Event Mapping (2) unchanged ...
   555→
   556→    // --- Transcript Storage (3 methods, core) ---
   557→    ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
   558→    ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
   559→    ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
   560→}
   561→
   562→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   563→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   564→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   565→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   566→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   567→    Agent
   568→    GetTranscriptPosition(sessionRef string) (int, error)
   569→    ExtractModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (files []string, newOffset int, err error)
   570→    ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   571→    ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   572→}
   573→```
   574→
   575→The dispatcher gracefully degrades:
   576→- With `TranscriptAnalyzer`: incremental transcript parsing, transcript-derived file lists, prompt/summary extraction
   577→- Without: git-status-only file detection, no prompt/summary metadata, transcript stored as opaque blob
   578→
   579→This reduces the core `Agent` interface from 14 to 10 methods while keeping the same functionality for agents that implement the full set.
   580→
   581→### Adjustment 3: Event.Metadata for agent-specific state
   582→
   583→**Problem**: Pi has tree-structured conversations with an `activeLeafId` that determines which branch is "active." This is runtime state (not persisted in the JSONL, confirmed via [Pi source](https://github.com/badlogic/pi-mono)). The Pi agent needs to pass this through the framework so it's available on subsequent calls. Cursor passes `is_background_agent` and `composer_mode` that could be useful for checkpoint metadata.
   584→
   585→**Adjustment**: Add a generic metadata field to `Event`:
   586→
   587→```go
   588→type Event struct {
   589→    // ... existing fields ...
   590→
   591→    // Agent-specific metadata the framework stores and passes back on subsequent events.
   592→    // Examples: Pi's activeLeafId, Cursor's is_background_agent.
   593→    Metadata map[string]string
   594→}
   595→```
   596→
   597→The framework stores this in session state and makes it available to subsequent `ParseHookEvent` calls (or transcript method calls) via context. This avoids widening the core interface for agent-specific concerns.
   598→
   599→### Agent-specific notes
   600→
   601→#### OpenCode
   602→
   603→Verified against [official docs](https://opencode.ai/docs/plugins/) and [source](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode).
   604→
   605→- **Plugin system**: `.opencode/plugins/entire.ts` with named export receiving `{ project, client, $, directory, worktree }` — well-documented, stable API
   606→- **Events**: `session.created`, `session.idle` (deprecated, use `session.status`), `tool.execute.before`, `tool.execute.after` — all confirmed
   607→- **Transcript access**: Via `client.session.messages()` SDK call, exported to temp JSONL — works but adds indirection (OpenCode stores sessions in SQLite, not JSONL files)
   608→- **Event mapping**: SessionStart (`session.created`), TurnEnd (`session.status` type=idle), SubagentStart/End (`tool.execute.before/after`). No TurnStart event exists — pre-prompt state capture not possible, git-status-only file detection
   609→- **Resume**: `opencode --session <id>` (not `--resume`)
   610→- **HookInstaller**: generates plugin file — fits cleanly
   611→
   612→#### Codex CLI
   613→
   614→Verified against [source](https://github.com/openai/codex) and [docs](https://developers.openai.com/codex/config-reference/).
   615→
   616→- **Current hook system**: Single `notify` event (`agent-turn-complete`) delivered via argv with `stdin(Stdio::null())`. Payload is legacy JSON with kebab-case fields (`thread-id`, `turn-id`, `input-messages`, `last-assistant-message`)
   617→- **Proper hook support in development**: The Codex team has confirmed that a richer hook system with stdin-based payload delivery and more lifecycle events is actively being built. Once shipped, Codex would support TurnStart, TurnEnd, SessionStart/End, etc. — mapping cleanly to our event model without the argv normalization workaround
   618→- **Transcript**: JSONL at `~/.codex/sessions/YYYY/MM/DD/rollout-*.jsonl` with typed `RolloutItem` discriminants (`session_meta`, `response_item`, `event_msg`, `compacted`, `turn_context`). Structured `file_change` events exist in the exec stream, and `patch_apply_begin`/`patch_apply_end` in internal EventMsg — no need for heuristic shell command parsing
   619→- **Config**: Both `~/.codex/config.toml` (global) and `.codex/config.toml` (project-local, requires trust) are supported
   620→- **Resume**: `codex resume <id>` (subcommand, not `--resume` flag)
   621→- **HookInstaller**: Modifies TOML `notify` array — fits cleanly
   622→
   623→#### Pi
   624→
   625→Verified against [source](https://github.com/badlogic/pi-mono) and [extension docs](https://github.com/badlogic/pi-mono/blob/main/packages/coding-agent/docs/extensions.md).
   626→
   627→- **Extension system**: `.pi/extensions/entire/index.ts` — auto-discovered, hot-reloaded, TypeScript. 26 total lifecycle events available.
   628→- **Events**: All 9 events used in PR #251 are confirmed real and documented. Richest event coverage of any contributed agent — maps to 6/7 of our event types (everything except Compaction, though `session_compact` exists and could be added)
   629→- **Tree-structured transcripts**: JSONL with `id`/`parentId` pointers forming a tree. `activeLeafId` is runtime state from `SessionManager.getLeafId()`, not persisted. Agent must reconstruct active branch via tree traversal — handled internally in `ExtractModifiedFiles`/`ExtractPrompts`, transparent to framework
   630→- **Resume**: `pi -c` (continue last), `pi -r` (interactive picker), `pi --session <path>` (specific session), or `/resume` in-TUI
   631→- **HookInstaller**: generates extension scaffold — fits cleanly
   632→- **Best-fit agent**: Pi maps most naturally to the TASK.md model. Full lifecycle events, accessible JSONL transcripts, clean extension API.
   633→
   634→#### Cursor
   635→
   636→Verified against [official hooks docs](https://cursor.com/docs/agent/hooks) and [third-party hooks docs](https://cursor.com/docs/agent/third-party-hooks).
   637→
   638→- **Hooks API**: `.cursor/hooks.json` with 20+ event types — richest hook surface of any agent. Includes `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`, `stop`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `subagentStart`, `subagentStop`, `preCompact`, `afterFileEdit`, and more. Maps to all 7 of our event types including Compaction and Subagent events.
   639→- **Payload delivery**: JSON on stdin, with universal fields on every hook (`conversation_id`, `generation_id`, `model`, `transcript_path`, `workspace_roots`, `user_email`). The `transcript_path` field is significant — Cursor provides the transcript file path in every hook payload.
   640→- **Transcript**: Format of the file at `transcript_path` is not publicly documented. Primary storage is SQLite (`state.vscdb`). The hook-provided transcript file is likely JSONL but this needs verification.
   641→- **Native Claude Code hook loading**: Cursor auto-discovers hooks from `.claude/settings.json` and translates event names (PascalCase → camelCase) and tool names (`Bash` → `Shell`, `Edit` → `Write`). This means `entire enable --agent claude-code` may already work in Cursor without a separate agent — **needs testing**. If the stdin payload format matches Claude's, no Cursor-specific agent is needed. If Cursor sends its own payload format (with `conversation_id`, `cursor_version`, etc.), a Cursor agent or detection layer is required.
   642→- **CLI**: `cursor-agent --resume <id>` (not `cursor --resume`)
   643→- **HookInstaller**: writes `.cursor/hooks.json` — fits cleanly
   644→- **Recommendation**: Before building a Cursor agent, test whether `entire enable --agent claude-code` works in Cursor via the native hook translation. If it does, document it. If not, build a Cursor agent that leverages the full 20+ event hook surface.
   645→
   646→---
   647→
   648→## Caveats
   649→
   650→### Cursor payload format ambiguity
   651→
   652→Cursor's third-party hooks documentation says it loads Claude Code hooks from `.claude/settings.json`, but it's unclear whether the **stdin payload** sent to those hooks matches Claude Code's format or Cursor's native format (which includes extra fields like `conversation_id`, `cursor_version`, `workspace_roots`). If Cursor sends its own format, the Claude Code agent's `ParseHookEvent` would fail to unmarshal the payload. This needs empirical testing before deciding whether a separate Cursor agent is necessary.
   653→
   654→### Codex hook system in transition
   655→
   656→The current Codex `notify` system is minimal (single event, argv delivery, fire-and-forget). A proper hook system with stdin payloads and richer lifecycle events is in active development by the Codex team. Our implementation should target the new hook system rather than building elaborate workarounds for the legacy `notify` mechanism. The argv normalization in Adjustment 1 is a stopgap — once the new hooks ship, Codex would use standard stdin delivery like every other agent.
   657→
   658→### OpenCode `session.idle` deprecation
   659→
   660→OpenCode's `session.idle` event is marked as deprecated in the source code (`packages/opencode/src/session/status.ts`). It still fires alongside `session.status`, but new integrations should use `session.status` and check `event.properties.status.type === "idle"`. The plugin should be updated before merging any OpenCode PR.
   661→
   662→### OpenCode transcript indirection
   663→
   664→OpenCode stores sessions in SQLite, not as filesystem JSONL files. The plugin must export transcripts via the SDK (`client.session.messages()`) to a temp JSONL file. This adds a layer of indirection and means the transcript is a point-in-time snapshot, not a live file. `GetTranscriptPosition` based on JSONL line offsets works, but the offset resets if the plugin re-exports the full transcript (which it does on every hook invocation in PR #341). Incremental offset tracking requires the plugin to append to the existing JSONL file rather than overwriting it.
   665→
   666→### Pi tree-structured transcripts
   667→
   668→Pi's `id`/`parentId` tree model means that naive line-based transcript parsing returns entries from **all branches**, not just the active one. The agent must reconstruct the active branch path by walking `parentId` pointers from the leaf. The `activeLeafId` is runtime state from `SessionManager.getLeafId()` and must be passed from the extension to the CLI (PR #251 does this via the hook payload's `active_leaf_id` field). If the leaf ID is lost (e.g., between sessions), the agent falls back to the last entry in the JSONL, which may be on a stale branch.
   669→
   670→### `TranscriptAnalyzer` degradation path
   671→
   672→When an agent doesn't implement `TranscriptAnalyzer`, the dispatcher must handle:
   673→- **File detection**: git-status-only (compare worktree against pre-prompt untracked baseline). This works well for new/deleted files but can't distinguish "files the agent modified" from "files modified by other processes."
   674→- **Prompts**: Empty — no prompt metadata in checkpoints. The `prompt.txt` file would be omitted or contain only the TurnStart event's `Prompt` field if available.
   675→- **Summary**: Empty — no summary in checkpoint metadata.
   676→- **Transcript position**: No incremental parsing. The full transcript is stored on every step. This increases storage but is functionally correct.
   677→- **Pre-prompt state**: `CapturePrePromptState` can still capture untracked files even without `GetTranscriptPosition`. The `TranscriptOffset` field would be 0, meaning the next `ExtractModifiedFiles` call (if the agent later implements `TranscriptAnalyzer`) processes the full transcript.
   678→

Whenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```

    31→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.

   138→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   139→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   140→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   141→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   142→	if err != nil {
   143→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   144→	}
   145→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   146→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   150→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   151→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   152→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   153→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   154→
   155→	// Copy transcript
   156→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   157→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   161→
   162→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   163→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   164→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   170→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   171→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   172→	var transcriptOffset int
   173→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   174→		transcriptOffset = preState.TranscriptOffset
   175→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   176→	} else {
   177→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   178→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   179→		if loadErr != nil {
   180→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   181→		}
   182→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   183→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   184→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   185→		}
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   189→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   190→	var transcriptLines []transcriptLine
   191→	var totalLines int
   192→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   193→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   194→		transcriptLines, totalLines, err = transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   195→		if err != nil {
   196→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   197→		}
   198→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcriptLines), totalLines)
   199→	} else {
   200→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   201→		// Use ParseFromFileAtLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   202→		transcriptLines, totalLines, err = transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   203→		if err != nil {
   204→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   209→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcriptLines)
   210→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   211→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   212→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   213→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   216→
   217→	// Extract summary
   218→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   219→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcriptLines)
   220→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   221→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   222→	}
   223→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   224→
   225→	// Get modified files from transcript (main agent + subagents).
   226→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   227→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), input.SessionID, "subagents")
   228→	modifiedFiles, subagentErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir)
   229→	if subagentErr != nil {
   230→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files with subagents: %v\n", subagentErr)
   231→		// Fall back to main transcript only
   232→		modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcriptLines)
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   236→	lastPrompt := ""
   237→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   238→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   239→	}
   240→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   241→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   242→
   243→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   244→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   245→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	if preState != nil {
   251→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   255→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   256→	if err != nil {
   257→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   261→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   262→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   263→	if changes != nil {
   264→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   265→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   269→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   270→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   271→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   272→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   273→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   274→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   275→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   276→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   277→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   278→		}
   279→		return nil
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   283→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   284→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   285→	}
   286→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   287→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   288→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   289→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   293→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   294→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   295→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Create context file before saving changes
   300→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   301→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcriptLines); err != nil {
   302→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   303→	}
   304→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   305→
   306→	// Get git author from local/global config
   307→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Get the configured strategy
   313→	strat := GetStrategy()
   314→
   315→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   316→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   317→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   318→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   322→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   323→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   324→	if preState != nil {
   325→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   326→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   330→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   331→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   332→		usage, tokenErr := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   333→		if tokenErr != nil {
   334→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   335→		} else {
   336→			tokenUsage = usage
   337→		}
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   341→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   342→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   343→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   344→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   345→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   346→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   347→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   348→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   349→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   350→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   351→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   352→		AgentType:                agentType,
   353→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   354→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   355→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   356→	}
   357→
   358→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   359→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   360→	}
   361→
   362→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   363→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   364→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   365→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   366→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   367→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   368→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   369→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   370→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   371→		if loadErr != nil {
   372→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   373→		}
   374→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   375→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   376→		if sessionState == nil {
   377→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   378→				SessionID: sessionID,
   379→			}
   380→		}
   381→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   382→		sessionState.StepCount++
   383→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   384→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   385→		} else {
   386→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   387→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   388→		}
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   392→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE.
   393→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   394→
   395→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   396→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   397→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	return nil
   401→}
   402→
   403→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   404→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   405→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   406→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   407→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   408→	if err != nil {
   409→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	// Get agent for logging context
   413→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   414→	if err != nil {
   415→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   419→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   420→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   421→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   422→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   423→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   424→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   425→	)
   426→
   427→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   428→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   429→	if !found {
   430→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   431→		return nil
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   435→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   436→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   437→		return nil
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   441→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   442→	if err != nil {
   443→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   444→		return nil
   445→	}
   446→
   447→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   448→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   449→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   450→		return nil
   451→	}
   452→
   453→	// Get git author
   454→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   455→	if err != nil {
   456→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   457→		return nil
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Get the active strategy
   461→	strat := GetStrategy()
   462→
   463→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   464→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   465→	if sessionID == "" {
   466→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   467→	}
   468→
   469→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   470→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   471→
   472→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   473→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   474→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   475→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   476→	if todoContent == "" {
   477→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   478→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   479→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   480→		if todoCount > 0 {
   481→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   482→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   483→		}
   484→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   485→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   489→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   490→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   491→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   492→	}
   493→
   494→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   495→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   496→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   497→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   498→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   499→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   500→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   501→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   502→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   503→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   504→		IsIncremental:       true,
   505→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   506→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   507→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   508→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   509→		AgentType:           agentType,
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   513→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   514→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   515→		return nil
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   519→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   520→	return nil
   521→}
   522→
   523→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   524→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   525→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	// Get agent for logging context
   531→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   532→	if err != nil {
   533→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   537→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   538→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   539→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   540→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   541→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   542→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   543→	)
   544→
   545→	// Log context to stdout
   546→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   547→
   548→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   549→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   550→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   551→	// in file changes.
   552→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   553→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	return nil
   557→}
   558→
   559→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   560→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   561→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   567→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   568→
   569→	// Get agent for logging context
   570→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   571→	if err != nil {
   572→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   573→	}
   574→
   575→	// Log parsed input context
   576→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   577→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   578→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   579→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   580→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   581→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   582→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   583→	)
   584→
   585→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   586→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   587→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   588→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   589→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   590→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   591→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   592→		}
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	// Log context to stdout
   596→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   597→
   598→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   599→	var modifiedFiles []string
   600→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   601→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   602→		parsed, _, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(subagentTranscriptPath, 0)
   603→		if parseErr != nil {
   604→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", parseErr)
   605→		} else {
   606→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(parsed)
   607→		}
   608→	} else {
   609→		// Fall back to main transcript
   610→		parsed, _, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(input.TranscriptPath, 0)
   611→		if parseErr != nil {
   612→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", parseErr)
   613→		} else {
   614→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(parsed)
   615→		}
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   619→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   620→	if err != nil {
   621→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   625→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   626→	if err != nil {
   627→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   631→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   632→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   633→	if err != nil {
   634→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   635→	}
   636→
   637→	// Filter and normalize paths
   638→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   639→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   640→	if changes != nil {
   641→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   642→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   646→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   647→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   648→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   649→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   650→		return nil
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   654→	mainLines, _, _ := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(input.TranscriptPath, 0) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   655→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, input.ToolUseID)
   656→
   657→	// Get git author
   658→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   659→	if err != nil {
   660→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	// Get the configured strategy
   664→	strat := GetStrategy()
   665→
   666→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   667→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   668→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   669→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   670→	}
   671→
   672→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   673→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   674→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   675→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   676→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   677→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   678→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   679→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   680→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   681→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   682→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   683→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   684→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   685→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   686→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   687→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   688→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   689→		AgentType:              agentType,
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   693→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   694→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   695→	}
   696→
   697→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   698→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   699→
   700→	return nil
   701→}
   702→
   703→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   704→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   705→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   706→}
   707→
   708→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   709→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   710→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   711→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   712→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   713→	if err != nil {
   714→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   715→	}
   716→
   717→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   718→	if err != nil {
   719→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   723→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   724→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   725→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   726→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   727→	)
   728→
   729→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   730→		return nil // No session to update
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   734→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   735→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   736→	}
   737→	return nil
   738→}
   739→
   740→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   741→// ACTIVE → IDLE. Best-effort: logs warnings on failure rather than
   742→// returning errors.
   743→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   744→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   745→	if loadErr != nil {
   746→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   747→		return
   748→	}
   749→	if turnState == nil {
   750→		return
   751→	}
   752→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   753→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   757→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   758→	strat := GetStrategy()
   759→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   760→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   761→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   762→		}
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   766→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   767→	}
   768→}
   769→
   770→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   771→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   772→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   773→	if err != nil {
   774→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   775→	}
   776→	if state == nil {
   777→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   778→	}
   779→
   780→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   781→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   782→	}
   783→
   784→	now := time.Now()
   785→	state.EndedAt = &now
   786→
   787→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   788→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   789→	}
   790→	return nil
   791→}
   792→
   793→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   794→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   795→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   796→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   797→
   798→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   799→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   800→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   801→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   802→//
   803→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   804→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   805→//
   806→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   807→// with whatever data is available.
   808→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   809→	const (
   810→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   811→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   812→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   813→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   814→	)
   815→
   816→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   817→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   818→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   819→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   820→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   821→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   822→			)
   823→			return
   824→		}
   825→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   826→	}
   827→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   828→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   829→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   830→	)
   831→}
   832→
   833→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   834→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   835→// close to hookStartTime.
   836→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   837→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   838→	if err != nil {
   839→		return false
   840→	}
   841→	defer f.Close()
   842→
   843→	// Seek to tail
   844→	info, err := f.Stat()
   845→	if err != nil {
   846→		return false
   847→	}
   848→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   849→	if offset < 0 {
   850→		offset = 0
   851→	}
   852→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   853→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   854→		return false
   855→	}
   856→
   857→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   858→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   859→	for _, line := range lines {
   860→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   861→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   862→			continue
   863→		}
   864→
   865→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   866→		var entry struct {
   867→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   868→		}
   869→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   870→			continue
   871→		}
   872→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   873→		if err != nil {
   874→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   875→			if err != nil {
   876→				continue
   877→			}
   878→		}
   879→
   880→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   881→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   882→			return true
   883→		}
   884→	}
   885→	return false
   886→}

     1→// hooks_geminicli_handlers.go contains Gemini CLI specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
