    21→// ErrSessionSkipped is returned when a session should be skipped (e.g., due to concurrent warning).
    22→var ErrSessionSkipped = errors.New("session skipped")
    23→
    24→// handleGeminiSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Gemini CLI.
    25→func handleGeminiSessionStart() error {
    26→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
    27→}
    28→
    29→// handleGeminiSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Gemini CLI.
    30→// This fires when the user explicitly exits the session (via "exit" or "logout" commands).
    31→// It parses the session-end input and marks the session as ended so that subsequent
    32→// git hooks (e.g., post-commit) can trigger condensation for ended sessions.
    33→func handleGeminiSessionEnd() error { // Parse stdin once upfront — all subsequent steps use ctx.sessionID
    34→	ctx, err := parseGeminiSessionEnd()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		if errors.Is(err, ErrSessionSkipped) {
    37→			return nil
    38→		}
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session-end input: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Mark session as ended using the parsed session ID
    43→	if err := markSessionEnded(ctx.sessionID); err != nil {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	return nil
    48→}
    49→
    50→// geminiSessionContext holds parsed session data for Gemini commits.
    51→type geminiSessionContext struct {
    52→	sessionID      string
    53→	transcriptPath string
    54→	sessionDir     string
    55→	sessionDirAbs  string
    56→	transcriptData []byte
    57→	allPrompts     []string
    58→	summary        string
    59→	modifiedFiles  []string
    60→	commitMessage  string
    61→}
    62→
    63→// parseGeminiSessionEnd parses the session-end hook input and validates transcript.
    64→func parseGeminiSessionEnd() (*geminiSessionContext, error) {
    65→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    76→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
    77→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
    78→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    79→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    80→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    81→	)
    82→
    83→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    84→	if sessionID == "" {
    85→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
    89→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	return &geminiSessionContext{
    94→		sessionID:      sessionID,
    95→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
    96→	}, nil
    97→}

    99→// setupGeminiSessionDir creates session directory and copies transcript.
   100→func setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   101→	ctx.sessionDir = paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(ctx.sessionID)
   102→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(ctx.sessionDir)
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		sessionDirAbs = ctx.sessionDir
   105→	}
   106→	ctx.sessionDirAbs = sessionDirAbs
   107→
   108→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   113→	if err := copyFile(ctx.transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   114→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   117→
   118→	transcriptData, err := os.ReadFile(ctx.transcriptPath)
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→	ctx.transcriptData = transcriptData
   123→
   124→	return nil
   125→}
   126→
   127→// extractGeminiMetadata extracts prompts, summary, and modified files from transcript.
   128→func extractGeminiMetadata(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   129→	allPrompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts(ctx.transcriptData)
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", err)
   132→	}
   133→	ctx.allPrompts = allPrompts
   134→
   135→	promptFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   136→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   137→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   138→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   141→
   142→	summary, err := geminicli.ExtractLastAssistantMessage(ctx.transcriptData)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", err)
   145→	}
   146→	ctx.summary = summary
   147→
   148→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   149→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   150→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   151→	}
   152→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   153→
   154→	modifiedFiles, err := geminicli.ExtractModifiedFiles(ctx.transcriptData)
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", err)
   157→	}
   158→	ctx.modifiedFiles = modifiedFiles
   159→
   160→	lastPrompt := ""
   161→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   162→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   163→	}
   164→	ctx.commitMessage = generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   165→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", ctx.commitMessage)
   166→
   167→	return nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→// commitGeminiSession commits the session changes using the strategy.
   171→func commitGeminiSession(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   172→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   180→	}
   181→	if preState != nil {
   182→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Loaded pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files, transcript offset: %d\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles), preState.TranscriptOffset)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state
   186→	var startMessageIndex int
   187→	if preState != nil {
   188→		startMessageIndex = preState.TranscriptOffset
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Calculate token usage for this prompt/response cycle (Gemini-specific)
   192→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   193→	if ctx.transcriptPath != "" {
   194→		usage, tokenErr := geminicli.CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(ctx.transcriptPath, startMessageIndex)
   195→		if tokenErr != nil {
   196→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   197→		} else if usage != nil && usage.APICallCount > 0 {
   198→			tokenUsage = usage
   199→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Token usage for this checkpoint: input=%d, output=%d, cache_read=%d, api_calls=%d\n",
   200→				tokenUsage.InputTokens, tokenUsage.OutputTokens, tokenUsage.CacheReadTokens, tokenUsage.APICallCount)
   201→		}
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   205→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(ctx.modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   211→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   212→	if changes != nil {
   213→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   214→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   218→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   219→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   220→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   221→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   222→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   223→		}
   224→		return nil
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   228→
   229→	contextFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   230→	if err := createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, ctx.commitMessage, ctx.sessionID, ctx.allPrompts, ctx.summary); err != nil {
   231→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   234→
   235→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   236→	if err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	strat := GetStrategy()
   241→
   242→	// Get agent type from the hook agent (determined by which hook command is running)
   243→	// This is authoritative - if we're in "entire hooks gemini session-end", it's Gemini CLI
   244→	hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   245→	if agentErr != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", agentErr)
   247→	}
   248→	agentType := hookAgent.Type()
   249→
   250→	// Get transcript identifier at start from pre-prompt state
   251→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   252→	if preState != nil {
   253→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	saveCtx := strategy.SaveContext{
   257→		SessionID:                ctx.sessionID,
   258→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   259→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   260→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   261→		MetadataDir:              ctx.sessionDir,
   262→		MetadataDirAbs:           ctx.sessionDirAbs,
   263→		CommitMessage:            ctx.commitMessage,
   264→		TranscriptPath:           ctx.transcriptPath,
   265→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   266→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   267→		AgentType:                agentType,
   268→		StepTranscriptStart:      startMessageIndex,
   269→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   270→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(saveCtx); err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   278→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session saved successfully\n")
   282→	return nil
   283→}
   284→
   285→// logFileChanges logs the modified, new, and deleted files to stderr.
   286→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   287→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   288→	for _, file := range modified {
   289→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   290→	}
   291→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   292→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   293→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   294→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   298→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   299→		for _, file := range deleted {
   300→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   301→		}
   302→	}
   303→}
   304→
   305→// createContextFileForGemini creates a context.md file for Gemini sessions.
   306→func createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   307→	var sb strings.Builder
   308→
   309→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   310→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Session ID: %s\n", sessionID))
   311→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   312→
   313→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   314→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   315→		for i, p := range prompts {
   316→			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p))
   317→		}
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	if summary != "" {
   321→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   322→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   323→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   327→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   328→	}
   329→	return nil
   330→}
   331→

   387→
   388→// handleGeminiBeforeAgent handles the BeforeAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   389→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's UserPromptSubmit - it fires when the user submits a prompt.
   390→// We capture the initial state here so we can track what files were modified during the session.
   391→// It also checks for concurrent sessions and blocks if another session has uncommitted changes.
   392→func handleGeminiBeforeAgent() error {
   393→	// Always use the Gemini agent for Gemini hooks (don't use GetAgent() which may
   394→	// return Claude based on auto-detection in environments like VSCode)
   395→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	// Parse hook input - BeforeAgent provides user prompt info similar to UserPromptSubmit
   401→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
   402→	if err != nil {
   403→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   407→
   408→	// Log with prompt if available (Gemini provides the user's prompt in BeforeAgent)
   409→	logArgs := []any{
   410→		slog.String("hook", "before-agent"),
   411→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   412→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   413→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   414→	}
   415→	if input.UserPrompt != "" {
   416→		// Truncate long prompts for logging
   417→		promptPreview := input.UserPrompt
   418→		if len(promptPreview) > 100 {
   419→			promptPreview = promptPreview[:100] + "..."
   420→		}
   421→		logArgs = append(logArgs, slog.String("prompt_preview", promptPreview))
   422→	}
   423→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-before-agent", logArgs...)
   424→
   425→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   426→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Capture pre-prompt state with transcript position (unified, uses TranscriptAnalyzer)
   430→	// This captures both untracked files and the current transcript message count
   431→	// so we can calculate token usage for just this prompt/response cycle
   432→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, input.SessionID, input.SessionRef); err != nil {
   433→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-prompt state: %w", err)
   434→	}
   435→
   436→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   437→	strat := GetStrategy()
   438→
   439→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (git hooks, gitignore, metadata branch).
   440→	// Done here at turn start so hooks are installed before any mid-turn commits.
   441→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   442→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   446→		agentType := ag.Type()
   447→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(input.SessionID, agentType, input.SessionRef, input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
   448→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   449→		}
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	return nil
   453→}
   454→
   455→// handleGeminiAfterAgent handles the AfterAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   456→// This fires after the agent has finished processing and generated a response.
   457→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's "Stop" hook - it commits the session changes with metadata.
   458→// AfterAgent fires after EVERY user prompt/response cycle, making it the correct place
   459→// for checkpoint creation (not SessionEnd, which only fires on explicit exit).
   460→func handleGeminiAfterAgent() error {
   461→	// Always use Gemini agent for Gemini hooks
   462→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   463→	if err != nil {
   464→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	// Parse hook input using HookStop - AfterAgent provides the same data as Stop
   468→	// (session_id, transcript_path) which is what we need for committing
   469→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   470→	if err != nil {
   471→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   472→	}
   473→
   474→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   475→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-after-agent",
   476→		slog.String("hook", "after-agent"),
   477→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   478→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   479→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   480→	)
   481→
   482→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   483→	if sessionID == "" {
   484→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   488→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   489→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   493→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   494→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   495→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	// Create session context and commit
   499→	ctx := &geminiSessionContext{
   500→		sessionID:      sessionID,
   501→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	if err := setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx); err != nil {
   505→		return err
   506→	}
   507→
   508→	if err := extractGeminiMetadata(ctx); err != nil {
   509→		return err
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	if err := commitGeminiSession(ctx); err != nil {
   513→		return err
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	// Transition session ACTIVE → IDLE (equivalent to Claude's transitionSessionTurnEnd)
   517→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   518→
   519→	return nil
   520→}

    18→
    19→// HookHandlerFunc is a function that handles a specific hook event.
    20→type HookHandlerFunc func() error
    21→
    22→// hookRegistry maps (agentName, hookName) to handler functions.
    23→// This allows agents to define their hook vocabulary while keeping
    24→// handler logic in the CLI package (avoiding circular dependencies).
    25→var hookRegistry = map[agent.AgentName]map[string]HookHandlerFunc{}
    26→
    27→// RegisterHookHandler registers a handler for an agent's hook.
    28→func RegisterHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string, handler HookHandlerFunc) {
    29→	if hookRegistry[agentName] == nil {
    30→		hookRegistry[agentName] = make(map[string]HookHandlerFunc)
    31→	}
    32→	hookRegistry[agentName][hookName] = handler
    33→}
    34→
    35→// GetHookHandler returns the handler for an agent's hook, or nil if not found.
    36→func GetHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) HookHandlerFunc {
    37→	if handlers, ok := hookRegistry[agentName]; ok {
    38→		return handlers[hookName]
    39→	}
    40→	return nil
    41→}
    42→

   151→		return handleGeminiAfterAgent()
   152→	})
   153→
   154→	RegisterHookHandler(agent.AgentNameGemini, geminicli.HookNameBeforeModel, func() error {
   155→		enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   156→		if err == nil && !enabled {
   157→			return nil
   158→		}
   159→		return handleGeminiBeforeModel()
   160→	})
   161→
   162→	RegisterHookHandler(agent.AgentNameGemini, geminicli.HookNameAfterModel, func() error {
   163→		enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   164→		if err == nil && !enabled {
   165→			return nil
   166→		}
   167→		return handleGeminiAfterModel()
   168→	})
   169→
   170→	RegisterHookHandler(agent.AgentNameGemini, geminicli.HookNameBeforeToolSelection, func() error {
   171→		enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   172→		if err == nil && !enabled {
   173→			return nil
   174→		}
   175→		return handleGeminiBeforeToolSelection()
   176→	})
   177→
   178→	RegisterHookHandler(agent.AgentNameGemini, geminicli.HookNamePreCompress, func() error {
   179→		enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   180→		if err == nil && !enabled {
   181→			return nil
   182→		}
   183→		return handleGeminiPreCompress()

   189→			return nil
   190→		}
   191→		return handleGeminiNotification()
   192→	})
   193→}
   194→
   195→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
   196→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
   197→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
   198→
   199→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
   200→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
   201→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
   202→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
   203→
   204→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
   205→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
   206→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
   207→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
   208→//
   209→
   210→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
   211→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
   212→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
   216→	if err != nil {
   217→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
   218→	}
   219→	return ag, nil
   220→}
   221→
   222→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
   223→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
   224→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
   225→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   226→		Use:    string(agentName),
   227→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
   228→		Hidden: true,
   229→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   230→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
   231→			return nil
   232→		},
   233→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   234→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
   235→				agentHookLogCleanup()
   236→			}
   237→			return nil
   238→		},
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
   242→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	return cmd
   246→}
   247→
   248→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
   249→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
   250→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
   251→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
   252→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
   253→	switch hookName {
   254→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
   255→		return "subagent"
   256→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
   257→		return "tool"
   258→	default:
   259→		return "agent"
   260→	}
   261→}
   262→
   263→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
   264→// It logs hook invocation at DEBUG level and completion with duration at INFO level.
   265→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
   266→	return &cobra.Command{
   267→		Use:    hookName,
   268→		Hidden: true,
   269→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
   270→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   271→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
   272→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   273→				return nil
   274→			}
   275→
   276→			start := time.Now()
   277→
   278→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   279→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   280→
   281→			// Get strategy name for logging
   282→			strategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase
   283→			strategyName = GetStrategy().Name()
   284→
   285→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   286→
   287→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   288→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   289→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   290→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   291→			)
   292→
   293→			handler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)
   294→			if handler == nil {
   295→				logging.Error(ctx, "no handler registered",
   296→					slog.String("hook", hookName),
   297→					slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   298→				)
   299→				return fmt.Errorf("no handler registered for %s/%s", agentName, hookName)
   300→			}
   301→
   302→			// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it
   303→			// without guessing from directory presence
   304→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   305→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   306→
   307→			hookErr := handler()
   308→
   309→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   310→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   311→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   312→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   313→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   314→			)
   315→
   316→			return hookErr
   317→		},
   318→	}
   319→}
   320→

    45→// parseTaskHookInput parses PreToolUse[Task] hook input from reader
    46→func parseTaskHookInput(r io.Reader) (*TaskHookInput, error) {
    47→	data, err := io.ReadAll(r)
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	if len(data) == 0 {
    53→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	var input TaskHookInput
    57→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &input); err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse JSON: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	return &input, nil
    62→}

   157→// ExtractTodoContentFromToolInput extracts the content of the in-progress todo item from TodoWrite tool_input.
   158→// Falls back to the first pending item if no in-progress item is found.

   161→// This function unwraps the outer tool_input object to extract the todos array,
   162→// then delegates to strategy.ExtractInProgressTodo for the actual parsing logic.
   163→func ExtractTodoContentFromToolInput(toolInput json.RawMessage) string {
   164→	if len(toolInput) == 0 {

   170→	if err := json.Unmarshal(toolInput, &input); err != nil {

   172→	}

   240→// handleSessionStartCommon is the shared implementation for session start hooks.
   241→// Used by both Claude Code and Gemini CLI handlers.
   242→func handleSessionStartCommon() error {
   243→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   254→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
   255→		slog.String("hook", "session-start"),
   256→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   257→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   258→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   259→	)
   260→
   261→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   262→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Build informational message
   266→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
   267→
   268→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
   269→	strat := GetStrategy()
   270→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
   271→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(input.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
   272→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
   273→		}
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Output informational message using agent-specific format
   277→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
   278→		return err
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
   282→	// This handles ENDED → IDLE (re-entering a session).
   283→	// TODO(ENT-221): dispatch ActionWarnStaleSession for ACTIVE sessions.
   284→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(input.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
   285→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
   286→	} else if state != nil {
   287→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   288→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   289→		}
   290→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   291→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   292→		}
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	return nil
   296→}
   297→
   298→// hookResponse represents a JSON response.
   299→// Used to control whether Agent continues processing the prompt.
   300→type hookResponse struct {
   301→	SystemMessage string `json:"systemMessage,omitempty"`
   302→}
   303→
   304→// outputHookResponse outputs a JSON response to stdout
   305→func outputHookResponse(reason string) error {
   306→	resp := hookResponse{
   307→		SystemMessage: reason,
   308→	}
   309→	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
   310→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
   311→	}
   312→	return nil
   313→}

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"time"
     6→)
     7→
     8→// EventType represents a normalized lifecycle event from any agent.
     9→// Agents translate their native hooks into these event types via ParseHookEvent.
    10→type EventType int
    11→
    12→const (
    13→	// SessionStart indicates the agent session has begun.
    14→	SessionStart EventType = iota + 1
    15→
    16→	// TurnStart indicates the user submitted a prompt and the agent is about to work.
    17→	TurnStart
    18→
    19→	// TurnEnd indicates the agent finished responding to a prompt.
    20→	TurnEnd
    21→
    22→	// Compaction indicates the agent is about to compress its context window.
    23→	// This triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd but also resets the transcript offset.
    24→	Compaction
    25→
    26→	// SessionEnd indicates the session has been terminated.
    27→	SessionEnd
    28→
    29→	// SubagentStart indicates a subagent (task) has been spawned.
    30→	SubagentStart
    31→
    32→	// SubagentEnd indicates a subagent (task) has completed.
    33→	SubagentEnd
    34→)
    35→
    36→// String returns a human-readable name for the event type.
    37→func (e EventType) String() string {
    38→	switch e {
    39→	case SessionStart:
    40→		return "SessionStart"
    41→	case TurnStart:
    42→		return "TurnStart"
    43→	case TurnEnd:
    44→		return "TurnEnd"
    45→	case Compaction:
    46→		return "Compaction"
    47→	case SessionEnd:
    48→		return "SessionEnd"
    49→	case SubagentStart:
    50→		return "SubagentStart"
    51→	case SubagentEnd:
    52→		return "SubagentEnd"
    53→	default:
    54→		return "Unknown"
    55→	}
    56→}
    57→
    58→// Event is a normalized lifecycle event produced by an agent's ParseHookEvent method.
    59→// The framework dispatcher uses these events to drive checkpoint/session lifecycle actions.
    60→type Event struct {
    61→	// Type is the kind of lifecycle event.
    62→	Type EventType
    63→
    64→	// SessionID identifies the agent session.
    65→	SessionID string
    66→
    67→	// PreviousSessionID is non-empty when this event represents a session continuation
    68→	// or handoff (e.g., Claude starting a new session ID after exiting plan mode).
    69→	PreviousSessionID string
    70→
    71→	// SessionRef is an agent-specific reference to the transcript (typically a file path).
    72→	SessionRef string
    73→
    74→	// Prompt is the user's prompt text (populated on TurnStart events).
    75→	Prompt string
    76→
    77→	// Timestamp is when the event occurred.
    78→	Timestamp time.Time
    79→
    80→	// ToolUseID identifies the tool invocation (for SubagentStart/SubagentEnd events).
    81→	ToolUseID string
    82→
    83→	// SubagentID identifies the subagent instance (for SubagentEnd events).
    84→	SubagentID string
    85→
    86→	// ToolInput is the raw tool input JSON (for subagent type/description extraction).
    87→	ToolInput json.RawMessage
    88→
    89→	// ResponseMessage is an optional message to display to the user via the agent.
    90→	ResponseMessage string
    91→
    92→	// Metadata holds agent-specific state that the framework stores and makes available
    93→	// on subsequent events. Examples: Pi's activeLeafId, Cursor's is_background_agent.
    94→	Metadata map[string]string
    95→}

    27→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.
    28→// This is the new Agent interface method; delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    29→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    30→	return c.GetHookNames()
    31→}
    32→
    33→// ParseHookEvent translates a Claude Code hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    34→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    35→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNamePreTask:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePostTask:
    48→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    49→	case HookNamePostTodo:
    50→		// PostTodo is Claude-specific; handled outside the generic dispatcher.
    51→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    52→	default:
    53→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    54→	}
    55→}
    56→
    57→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    58→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    59→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→	return data, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    67→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    68→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	var prompts []string
    74→	for i := range lines {
    75→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    76→			continue
    77→		}
    78→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    79→		if content != "" {
    80→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→	return prompts, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    87→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    88→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    94→	if parseErr != nil {
    95→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Walk backward to find last assistant text block
    99→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   100→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   101→			continue
   102→		}
   103→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   108→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   109→				return block.Text, nil
   110→			}
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return "", nil
   114→}
   115→

   119→	waitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())
   120→	return nil
   121→}

   127→	// which may contain the session ID in its directory structure.
   128→	// For now, compute subagentsDir from the transcript path structure.

   130→}

   221→// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   222→func readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   223→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   224→	if err != nil {
   225→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   226→	}
   227→	if len(data) == 0 {
   228→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   229→	}
   230→	var result T
   231→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   232→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   233→	}
   234→	return &result, nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   238→
   239→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   240→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   241→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   242→
   243→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   244→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   245→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   246→	const (
   247→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   248→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   249→		tailBytes    = 4096
   250→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   251→	)
   252→
   253→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   254→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   255→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   256→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   257→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   258→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   259→			)
   260→			return
   261→		}
   262→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   263→	}
   264→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   265→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   266→	)
   267→}
   268→
   269→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   270→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   271→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		return false
   274→	}
   275→	defer f.Close()
   276→
   277→	info, err := f.Stat()
   278→	if err != nil {
   279→		return false
   280→	}
   281→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   282→	if offset < 0 {
   283→		offset = 0
   284→	}
   285→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   286→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   287→		return false
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   291→	for _, line := range lines {
   292→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   293→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   294→			continue
   295→		}
   296→
   297→		var entry struct {
   298→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   299→		}
   300→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   301→			continue
   302→		}
   303→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   304→		if err != nil {
   305→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   306→			if err != nil {
   307→				continue
   308→			}
   309→		}
   310→		cutoff := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   311→		if ts.After(cutoff) {
   312→			return true
   313→		}
   314→	}
   315→	return false
   316→}
   317→

    29→	switch hookName {

  "content": "// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.\n// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.\n//\n// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from \"agent handler calls framework functions\"\n// to \"framework dispatcher calls agent methods.\" Agents are passive data providers;\n// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,\n// file change detection, metadata generation.\npackage cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n)\n\n// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.\n// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.\nfunc DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tswitch event.Type {\n\tcase agent.SessionStart:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)\n\tcase agent.TurnStart:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)\n\tcase agent.TurnEnd:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)\n\tcase agent.Compaction:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)\n\tcase agent.SessionEnd:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)\n\tcase agent.SubagentStart:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)\n\tcase agent.SubagentEnd:\n\t\treturn handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"unknown lifecycle event type: %d\", event.Type)\n\t}\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,\n// fires state machine transition.\nfunc handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"session-start\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_ref\", event.SessionRef),\n\t)\n\n\tif event.SessionID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"no session_id in %s event\", event.Type)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build informational message\n\tmessage := \"\\n\\nPowered by Entire:\\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit.\"\n\n\t// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any\n\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\tif concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {\n\t\tif count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {\n\t\t\tmessage += fmt.Sprintf(\"\\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.\", count)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Output informational message\n\tif event.ResponseMessage != \"\" {\n\t\tmessage = event.ResponseMessage\n\t}\n\tif err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).\n\tif state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\\n\", loadErr)\n\t} else if state != nil {\n\t\tif transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: session start transition failed: %v\\n\", transErr)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\\n\", saveErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,\n// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.\nfunc handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"turn-start\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_ref\", event.SessionRef),\n\t)\n\n\tsessionID := event.SessionID\n\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"no session_id in %s event\", event.Type)\n\t}\n\n\t// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)\n\tif err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session\n\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\n\tif err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {\n\t\tagentType := ag.Type()\n\t\tif err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,\n// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.\nfunc handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error { //nolint:maintidx // consolidated from two large handlers\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"turn-end\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_ref\", event.SessionRef),\n\t)\n\n\tsessionID := event.SessionID\n\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\tsessionID = unknownSessionID\n\t}\n\n\ttranscriptRef := event.SessionRef\n\tif transcriptRef == \"\" || !fileExists(transcriptRef) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"transcript file not found or empty: %s\", transcriptRef)\n\t}\n\n\t// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.\n\tif repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, \"Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.\")\n\t\treturn NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create session metadata directory\n\tsessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)\n\tsessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tsessionDirAbs = sessionDir\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create session directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready\n\tif preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {\n\t\tif err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Copy transcript to session directory\n\ttranscriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tlogFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Copied transcript to: %s\\n\", sessionDir+\"/\"+paths.TranscriptFileName)\n\n\t// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)\n\tpreState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine transcript offset\n\ttranscriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)\n\n\t// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)\n\tvar allPrompts []string\n\tvar summary string\n\tvar modifiedFiles []string\n\tvar newTranscriptPosition int\n\n\tif analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {\n\t\t// Extract prompts\n\t\tif prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\\n\", promptErr)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tallPrompts = prompts\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Extract summary\n\t\tif s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\\n\", sumErr)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tsummary = s\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Extract modified files (agent handles its own format)\n\t\tif files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\\n\", fileErr)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tmodifiedFiles = files\n\t\t\tnewTranscriptPosition = pos\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Write prompts file\n\tpromptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)\n\tpromptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, \"\\n\\n---\\n\\n\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write prompt file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\\n\", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+\"/\"+paths.PromptFileName)\n\n\t// Write summary file\n\tsummaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write summary file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Extracted summary to: %s\\n\", sessionDir+\"/\"+paths.SummaryFileName)\n\n\t// Generate commit message from last prompt\n\tlastPrompt := \"\"\n\tif len(allPrompts) > 0 {\n\t\tlastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]\n\t}\n\tcommitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Using commit message: %s\\n\", commitMessage)\n\n\t// Get repo root for path normalization\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repo root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif preState != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\\n\", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))\n\t}\n\n\t// Detect file changes via git status\n\tchanges, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter and normalize all paths\n\trelModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)\n\tvar relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string\n\tif changes != nil {\n\t\trelNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)\n\t\trelDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if there are any changes\n\ttotalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)\n\tif totalChanges == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"No files were modified during this session\\n\")\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Skipping commit\\n\")\n\t\ttransitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)\n\t\tif cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\\n\", cleanupErr)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Log file changes\n\tlogFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)\n\n\t// Create context file\n\tcontextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)\n\tif err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create context file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Created context file: %s\\n\", sessionDir+\"/\"+paths.ContextFileName)\n\n\t// Get git author\n\tauthor, err := GetGitAuthor()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get git author: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get strategy and agent type\n\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\tagentType := ag.Type()\n\n\t// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state\n\tvar transcriptIdentifierAtStart string\n\tvar transcriptLinesAtStart int\n\tif preState != nil {\n\t\ttranscriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier\n\t\ttranscriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset\n\t}\n\n\t// Calculate token usage (optional interface)\n\tvar tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage\n\tif calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {\n\t\tusage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)\n\t\tif tokenErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\\n\", tokenErr)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttokenUsage = usage\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy\n\tctx := strategy.SaveContext{\n\t\tSessionID:                sessionID,\n\t\tModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,\n\t\tNewFiles:                 relNewFiles,\n\t\tDeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,\n\t\tMetadataDir:              sessionDir,\n\t\tMetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,\n\t\tCommitMessage:            commitMessage,\n\t\tTranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,\n\t\tAuthorName:               author.Name,\n\t\tAuthorEmail:              author.Email,\n\t\tAgentType:                agentType,\n\t\tStepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,\n\t\tStepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,\n\t\tTokenUsage:               tokenUsage,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to save changes: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy\n\tif strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {\n\t\tupdateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)\n\t}\n\n\t// Transition session phase and cleanup\n\ttransitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)\n\tif cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\\n\", cleanupErr)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves like TurnEnd,\n// then resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.\nfunc handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\t// Compaction triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd\n\tif err := handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Reset transcript offset in session state since the transcript may be reorganized\n\tsessionState, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state for compaction reset: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif sessionState != nil {\n\t\tsessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0\n\t\tif saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to reset transcript offset after compaction: %v\\n\", saveErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.\nfunc handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"session-end\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t)\n\n\tif event.SessionID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil // No session to update\n\t}\n\n\tif err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.\nfunc handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"subagent-start\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"tool_use_id\", event.ToolUseID),\n\t)\n\n\t// Log context\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"[entire] Subagent started\\n\")\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Session ID: %s\\n\", event.SessionID)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Tool Use ID: %s\\n\", event.ToolUseID)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Transcript: %s\\n\", event.SessionRef)\n\n\t// Capture pre-task state\n\tif err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to capture pre-task state: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.\nfunc handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"subagent-end\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"tool_use_id\", event.ToolUseID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"subagent_id\", event.SubagentID),\n\t)\n\n\t// Extract subagent type and description from tool input\n\tsubagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)\n\n\t// Determine subagent transcript path\n\ttranscriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)\n\tvar subagentTranscriptPath string\n\tif event.SubagentID != \"\" {\n\t\tsubagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)\n\t\tif !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {\n\t\t\tsubagentTranscriptPath = \"\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Log context\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"[entire] Subagent completed\\n\")\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Session ID: %s\\n\", event.SessionID)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Tool Use ID: %s\\n\", event.ToolUseID)\n\tif event.SubagentID != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Agent ID: %s\\n\", event.SubagentID)\n\t}\n\tif subagentTranscriptPath != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, \"  Subagent Transcript: %s\\n\", subagentTranscriptPath)\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract modified files from subagent transcript\n\tvar modifiedFiles []string\n\tif analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {\n\t\ttranscriptToScan := event.SessionRef\n\t\tif subagentTranscriptPath != \"\" {\n\t\t\ttranscriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath\n\t\t}\n\t\tif files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\\n\", fileErr)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tmodifiedFiles = files\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Load pre-task state and detect file changes\n\tpreState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\tchanges, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get repo root and normalize paths\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repo root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trelModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)\n\tvar relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string\n\tif changes != nil {\n\t\trelNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)\n\t\trelDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)\n\t}\n\n\t// If no changes, skip\n\tif len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\\n\")\n\t\t_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)\n\tvar checkpointUUID string\n\tif analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {\n\t\t// Use ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset on the main transcript to get position info\n\t\t// and then look for checkpoint UUID via the legacy path\n\t\t_ = analyzer // UUID lookup requires transcript parsing — delegate to existing helper\n\t}\n\t// Fall back to the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder\n\tmainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort\n\tif mainLines != nil {\n\t\tcheckpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get git author\n\tauthor, err := GetGitAuthor()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get git author: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build task checkpoint context\n\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\tagentType := ag.Type()\n\n\tctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{\n\t\tSessionID:              event.SessionID,\n\t\tToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,\n\t\tAgentID:                event.SubagentID,\n\t\tModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,\n\t\tNewFiles:               relNewFiles,\n\t\tDeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,\n\t\tTranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,\n\t\tSubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,\n\t\tCheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,\n\t\tAuthorName:             author.Name,\n\t\tAuthorEmail:            author.Email,\n\t\tSubagentType:           subagentType,\n\t\tTaskDescription:        taskDescription,\n\t\tAgentType:              agentType,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to save task checkpoint: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// --- Helper functions ---\n\n// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.\n// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.\nfunc resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {\n\tif preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\\n\", preState.TranscriptOffset)\n\t\treturn preState.TranscriptOffset\n\t}\n\n\t// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)\n\tsessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif loadErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state: %v\\n\", loadErr)\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tif sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\\n\", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)\n\t\treturn sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart\n\t}\n\n\treturn 0\n}\n\n// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position\n// for the auto-commit strategy.\nfunc updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {\n\tsessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif loadErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state: %v\\n\", loadErr)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif sessionState == nil {\n\t\tsessionState = &strategy.SessionState{\n\t\t\tSessionID: sessionID,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tsessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition\n\tsessionState.StepCount++\n\tif updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to update session state: %v\\n\", updateErr)\n\t} else {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\\n\",\n\t\t\tnewPosition, sessionState.StepCount)\n\t}\n}\n\n// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.\n// This is a unified version that works for all agents.\nfunc createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\n\tsb.WriteString(\"# Session Context\\n\\n\")\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Session ID: %s\\n\", sessionID))\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Commit Message: %s\\n\\n\", commitMessage))\n\n\tif len(prompts) > 0 {\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"## Prompts\\n\\n\")\n\t\tfor i, p := range prompts {\n\t\t\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"### Prompt %d\\n\\n%s\\n\\n\", i+1, p))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif summary != \"\" {\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"## Summary\\n\\n\")\n\t\tsb.WriteString(summary)\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write context file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.\n// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.\nfunc parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {\n\treturn parseTranscriptFromFile(transcriptPath, 0)\n}\n"
}

For Phase 2c, I need to modify `newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()` in `hook_registry.go` to use `ParseHookEvent` + `DispatchLifecycleEvent`. The key change: instead of looking up handlers in the `hookRegistry` map, use the agent's `ParseHookEvent` to get a normalized event, then dispatch it.

err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\n\t\t\t// Initialize logging context with agent name\n\t\t\tctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"hooks\"), agentName)\n\n\t\t\t// Get strategy name for logging\n\t\t\tstrategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase\n\t\t\tstrategyName = GetStrategy().Name()\n\n\t\t\thookType := getHookType(hookName)\n\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"hook invoked\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\thandler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)\n\t\t\tif handler == nil {\n\t\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"no handler registered\",\n\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"no handler registered for %s/%s\", agentName, hookName)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it\n\t\t\t// without guessing from directory presence\n\t\t\tcurrentHookAgentName = agentName\n\t\t\tdefer func() { currentHookAgentName = \"\" }()\n\n\t\t\thookErr := handler()\n\n\t\t\tlogging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, \"hook completed\", start,\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", hookErr == nil),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\treturn hookErr\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.\n// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path,\n// falling back to the legacy handler registry for hooks that return nil events (e.g., PostTodo).\nfunc newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    hookName,\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tShort:  \"Called on \" + hookName,\n\t\tRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\t// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working\n\t\t\tif _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Skip if Entire is not enabled\n\t\t\tenabled, err := IsEnabled()\n\t\t\tif err == nil && !enabled {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\n\t\t\t// Initialize logging context with agent name\n\t\t\tctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"hooks\"), agentName)\n\n\t\t\t// Get strategy name for logging\n\t\t\tstrategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase\n\t\t\tstrategyName = GetStrategy().Name()\n\n\t\t\thookType := getHookType(hookName)\n\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"hook invoked\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\t// Set the current hook agent so legacy handlers can retrieve it\n\t\t\tcurrentHookAgentName = agentName\n\t\t\tdefer func() { currentHookAgentName = \"\" }()\n\n\t\t\t// Get the agent and try the lifecycle dispatcher path\n\t\t\tag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)\n\t\t\tif agentErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get agent: %w\", agentErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tevent, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, os.Stdin)\n\t\t\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hook event: %w\", parseErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tvar hookErr error\n\t\t\tif event != nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher\n\t\t\t\thookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t// No lifecycle event — fall back to registered handler (e.g., PostTodo)\n\t\t\t\thandler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)\n\t\t\t\tif handler != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\thookErr = handler()\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// If no handler registered either, silently do nothing\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tlogging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, \"hook completed\", start,\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", hookErr == nil),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\treturn hookErr\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}"
}

   331→// writeTestSessionState creates a session state file in .git/entire-sessions/ for testing.
   332→func writeTestSessionState(t *testing.T, repoDir, sessionID string) {
   333→	t.Helper()
   334→	stateDir := filepath.Join(repoDir, ".git", session.SessionStateDirName)
   335→	if err := os.MkdirAll(stateDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   336→		t.Fatalf("failed to create session state directory: %v", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	now := time.Now()
   340→	state := session.State{
   341→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   342→		StartedAt:           now,
   343→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   344→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   345→	}
   346→	data, err := json.Marshal(state)
   347→	if err != nil {
   348→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal state: %v", err)
   349→	}
   350→	stateFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   351→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   352→		t.Fatalf("failed to write session state file: %v", err)
   353→	}
   354→	t.Cleanup(func() { os.Remove(stateFile) })
   355→}

The tests use `agent.AgentName("test-agent")` which isn't registered in the agent registry. I need to handle the case where `agent.Get()` fails by falling back to the old handler system.

  "new_string": "\t\t\t// Try the lifecycle dispatcher path: ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent.\n\t\t\t// Falls back to legacy handler registry if agent is unresolved or event is nil.\n\t\t\tvar hookErr error\n\t\t\tag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)\n\t\t\tif agentErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\tevent, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, os.Stdin)\n\t\t\t\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hook event: %w\", parseErr)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif event != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher\n\t\t\t\t\thookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\t// No lifecycle event — fall back to registered handler (e.g., PostTodo)\n\t\t\t\t\thandler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)\n\t\t\t\t\tif handler != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\thookErr = handler()\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t// Agent not found — fall back to legacy handler registry\n\t\t\t\thandler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)\n\t\t\t\tif handler == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"no handler for %s/%s and agent not found: %w\", agentName, hookName, agentErr)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\thookErr = handler()\n\t\t\t}"
}

> AGENT

Here's a summary of everything completed in Phase 2:

**Phase 2a: Unify PrePromptState capture** (completed)
- Unified `CapturePrePromptState` and `CaptureGeminiPrePromptState` into a single function taking `agent.Agent`
- Added `TranscriptOffset` field to `PrePromptState`, deprecated old fields (`StepTranscriptStart`, `StartMessageIndex`, `LastTranscriptLineCount`)
- Added backward-compat migration in `normalizePrePromptState()`
- Fixed off-by-one bug in `ClaudeCodeAgent.GetTranscriptPosition()` — wasn't counting final line without trailing newline

**Phase 2b: Build generic lifecycle dispatcher** (completed)
- Created `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` with `DispatchLifecycleEvent()` — single entry point that routes normalized events to handler functions
- 7 handler functions consolidate duplicated framework logic from Claude and Gemini handlers:
  - `handleLifecycleSessionStart` — banner, concurrent session check, state machine
  - `handleLifecycleTurnStart` — pre-prompt state capture, strategy setup, session init
  - `handleLifecycleTurnEnd` — the main flow: transcript prep/copy, metadata extraction via agent interfaces (`TranscriptAnalyzer`, `TranscriptPreparer`, `TokenCalculator`), file change detection, save step + checkpoint
  - `handleLifecycleCompaction` — delegates to TurnEnd, resets transcript offset
  - `handleLifecycleSessionEnd` — marks session ended
  - `handleLifecycleSubagentStart/End` — pre-task state capture, task checkpoint

**Phase 2c: Hook command integration** (completed)
- Updated `newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()` in `hook_registry.go` to use `ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent` as primary path
- Falls back to legacy handler registry for nil events (PostTodo) and unresolved agents (test agents)
- Moved `IsEnabled()` check into the command function (previously in each handler wrapper)
- All existing tests pass, including integration tests with race detection
